        const cBlend = Math.min(1, (distFromCamp - 7.5) / 14.0);\n            const campSmooth = cBlend * cBlend * (3 - 2 * cBlend);\n\n            // Glatki prelaz oko koridora za poletanje aviona (bez oštrog stepenika!)\n            let corridorSmooth = 1.0;\n            if (z < 2 && z > -70) {\n                const distFromCorridor = Math.abs(x + 7.5);\n                if (distFromCorridor <= 5.2) return 0;\n                const ct = Math.min(1, (distFromCorridor - 5.2) / 11.0);\n                corridorSmooth = ct * ct * (3 - 2 * ct);\n            }\n\n            const gentleRidge = Math.exp(-Math.pow((z + 29.0) / 17.0, 2)) * 2.05;\n            const rollingSlope = Math.sin(x * 0.035 + 0.4) * Math.cos(z * 0.032 - 0.2) * 1.35;\n            const softUndulation = Math.cos(x * 0.062 - z * 0.052) * 0.42;\n            const outerRim = Math.max(0, (Math.hypot(x, z) - 66) * 0.14);\n            return Math.max(0, (gentleRidge + rollingSlope + softUndulation + outerRim + 0.52) * campSmooth * corridorSmooth);\n        }\n\n        // ============================================================================\n        // 2. flashlight.shadow.bias = -0.0003;\n            flashlight.target = new THREE.Object3D();\n            scene.add(hemiLight, ambientLight, moonLight, rimMoonLight, flashlight, flashlight.target);\n            lightningLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xe7f5ff, 0);\n            lightningLight.position.set(16, 34, -10);\n            scene.add(lightningLight);\n            buildArcticSky();\n\n            // Šejder materijali za snežno tlo i stene\n            floorMat = createCustomShader(true);\n            treeMat = createCustomShader(false);\n\n            // Glatko snežno ostrvsko tlo sa blagim varijacijama visine (144x144 segmenata)\n            const islandGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(440, 440, 144, 144);\n            const posAttr = islandGeo.attributes.position;\n            for (let i = 0; i < posAttr.count; i++) {\n                const vx = posAttr.getX(i);\n                const vy = posAttr.getY(i);\n                posAttr.setZ(i, getTerrainHeight(vx, -vy));\n            }\n            islandGeo.computeVertexNormals();\n            const islandMesh = new THREE.Mesh(islandGeo, floorMat);\n            islandMesh.rotation.x = -PI / 2;\n            islandMesh.receiveShadow = true;\n            scene.add(islandMesh);\n\n            // Sloj za prijem dinamičkih senki drveća, likova i aviona preko snežnog terena\n            const shadowOverlayMat = new THREE.ShadowMaterial({\n                color: 0x3468a8,\n                opacity: 0.44,\n                transparent: true,\n                depthWrite: false,\n                polygonOffset: true,\n                polygonOffsetFactor: -1,\n                polygonOffsetUnits: -1\n            });\n            const islandShadowMesh = new THREE.Mesh(islandGeo, shadowOverlayMat);\n            islandShadowMesh.rotation.x = -PI / 2;\n            islandShadowMesh.position.y = 0.015;\n            islandShadowMesh.receiveShadow = true;\n            scene.add(islandShadowMesh);\n\n            // Snežno-ledena ravnica ka Vukovoj bazi na severu\n            const seaGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2000, 2000);\n            const seaMesh = new THREE.Mesh(seaGeo, floorMat);\n            seaMesh.rotation.x = -PI / 2;\n            seaMesh.position.set(0, -0.35, -420);\n            scene.add(seaMesh);\n\n            buildArcticCinematicEnvironment();\n            buildForestAndRocks();\n            buildBlizzardParticles();\n            buildRepairableAircraft();\n            buildCampfireAndCompanions();\n            spawnAircraftPartsAndSupplies();\n            spawnForestSilhouettes();\n            buildVukNorthernBase();\n            ark2BuildWorld();\n            createHangarSet();\n            createCinemaCabin();\n            createAftermathSet();\n            enhanceCinematicSets();\n            createCinematicAtmo();\n            arkInitWorldDetail();\n\n            window.addEventListener('resize', () => {\n                camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;\n                camera.updateProjectionMatrix();\n                renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);\n            });\n        }\n\n        function buildArcticSky() {\n            // 1) Velika proceduralna nebeska kupola: čisto jarko plavo nebo sa mekim belim oblacima nošenim vetrom (kao na referentnoj slici)\n            const skyDomeGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(1100, 48, 32);\n            const skyDomeMat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({\n                side: THREE.BackSide,\n                depthWrite: false,\n                fog: false,\n                uniforms: {\n                    uTime: sharedUniforms.time\n                },\n                vertexShader: `\n                    varying vec3 vWorldDir;\n                    void main() {\n                        vec4 wp = modelMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);\n                        vWorldDir = normalize(position);\n                        gl_Position = projectionMatrix * viewMatrix * wp;\n                    }\n                `,\n                fragmentShader: `\n                    varying vec3 vWorldDir;\n                    uniform float uTime;\n                    float hash21(vec2 p){return fract(sin(dot(p,vec2(127.1,311.7)))*43758.5453123);}\n                    float noise21(vec2 p){vec2 i=floor(p),f=fract(p);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);return mix(mix(hash21(i),hash21(i+vec2(1,0)),f.x),mix(hash21(i+vec2(0,1)),hash21(i+vec2(1,1)),f.x),f.y);}\n                    float fBm(vec2 p){return noise21(p)*.54+noise21(p*2.05)*.28+noise21(p*4.15)*.18;}\n                    void main() {\n                        vec3 d = normalize(vWorldDir);\n                        float h = clamp(d.y, 0.0, 1.0);\n\n                        // Jarko azurno-plavo nebo u gornjem delu i svetlo-ledeni horizont\n                        vec3 horizonCol = vec3(0.44, 0.64, 0.81);\n                        vec3 lowSkyCol  = vec3(0.18, 0.40, 0.65);\n                        vec3 midSkyCol  = vec3(0.075, 0.22, 0.49);\n                        vec3 zenithCol  = vec3(0.025, 0.08, 0.25);\n                        vec3 sky = mix(horizonCol, lowSkyCol, smoothstep(0.0, 0.12, h));\n                        sky = mix(sky, midSkyCol, smoothstep(0.08, 0.34, h));\n                        sky = mix(sky, zenithCol, smoothstep(0.32, 0.80, h));\n\n                        // Stilizovani, izduženi meki beli oblaci preko gornjeg i srednjeg neba (po uzoru na referentnu slici)\n                        if (d.y > 0.01) {\n                            vec2 skyUV = d.xz / (d.y + 0.20) * 1.15;\n                            vec2 wind = vec2(uTime * 0.005, -uTime * 0.002);\n                            vec2 stretchedUV = vec2(skyUV.x * 0.75, skyUV.y * 1.35);\n                            float w1 = fBm(stretchedUV * 1.10 + wind);\n                            float w2 = fBm(stretchedUV * 2.35 - wind * 1.2 + vec2(w1 * 0.8, w1 * 0.4));\n                            float ribbon = sin(skyUV.x * 1.8 - skyUV.y * 1.2 + w1 * 4.2) * 0.5 + 0.5;\n                            float cloudField = w1 * 0.50 + w2 * 0.32 + ribbon * 0.18;\n\n                            float elevMask = smoothstep(0.02, 0.12, h) * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.62, 0.92, h) * 0.20);\n                            float cloudAlpha = smoothstep(0.51, 0.70, cloudField) * elevMask;\n                            float cloudLit = smoothstep(0.54, 0.75, w2 * 0.65 + ribbon * 0.35);\n\n                            vec3 cloudUnder = vec3(0.28, 0.43, 0.61);\n                            vec3 cloudTop   = vec3(0.66, 0.78, 0.88);\n                            vec3 cloudCol   = mix(cloudUnder, cloudTop, cloudLit);\n                            sky = mix(sky, cloudCol, cloudAlpha * 0.92);\n                        }\n\n                        gl_FragColor = vec4(sky, 1.0);\n                    }\n                `\n            });\n            const skyDome = new THREE.Mesh(skyDomeGeo, skyDomeMat);\n            scene.add(skyDome);\n\n            // 2) Tanki visoki pramenovi oblaka u daljini\n            for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {\n                const geo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(340, 48, 32, 6);\n                const mat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({\n                    transparent: true,\n                    depthWrite: false,\n                    fog: false,\n                    side: THREE.DoubleSide,\n                    uniforms: { uTime: sharedUniforms.time, uLayer: { value: i } },\n                    vertexShader: `varying vec2 vUv;void main(){vUv=uv;gl_Position=projectionMatrix*modelViewMatrix*vec4(position,1.);}`,\n                    fragmentShader: `\n                        varying vec2 vUv;\n                        uniform float uTime;\n                        uniform float uLayer;\n                        void main(){\n                            float wave = sin(vUv.x * 7.0 + uTime * 0.08 + uLayer * 1.7) * 0.5 + 0.5;\n                            float edgeX = smoothstep(0.0, 0.25, vUv.x) * smoothstep(1.0, 0.75, vUv.x);\n                            float edgeY = pow(sin(vUv.y * 3.14159265), 2.0);\n                            float alpha = edgeX * edgeY * (0.12 + 0.18 * wave);\n                            gl_FragColor = vec4(0.96, 0.99, 1.0, alpha);\n                        }\n                    `\n                });\n                const sheet = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);\n                sheet.position.set(-40 + i * 30, 118 + i * 12, -260 - i * 32);\n                sheet.rotation.y = -0.08 + i * 0.06;\n                sheet.rotation.x = 0.18;\n                scene.add(sheet);\n                auroras.push(sheet);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Veliki alpski planinski venac sa per-pixel GLSL šejderom za stenovite litice, osunčane snežne grebene i izmaglicu u podnožju (kao na referentnoj slici)\n        let arcticVisualEnv = null, arcticIceShards = null, arcticSnowdrifts = null;\n        function buildArcticCinematicEnvironment() {\n            arcticVisualEnv = new THREE.Group();\n            scene.add(arcticVisualEnv);\n\n            const d = new THREE.Object3D();\n            let seeded = 128724;\n            const rnd = () => ((seeded = (seeded * 1664525 + 1013904223) >>> 0) / 4294967296);\n\n            const hash2 = (x, z) => {\n                const s = Math.sin(x * 127.1 + z * 311.7) * 43758.5453;\n                return s - Math.floor(s);\n            };\n            const valNoise = (x, z) => {\n                const ix = Math.floor(x), iz = Math.floor(z);\n                const fx = x - ix, fz = z - iz;\n                const ux = fx * fx * (3 - 2 * fx), uz = fz * fz * (3 - 2 * fz);\n                const a = hash2(ix, iz), b = hash2(ix + 1, iz);\n                const c = hash2(ix, iz + 1), d2 = hash2(ix + 1, iz + 1);\n                return (a + (b - a) * ux) + ((c - a) + (a - b - c + d2) * ux) * uz;\n            };\n            const ridgeNoise = (x, z) => {\n                const r1 = 1.0 - Math.abs(valNoise(x, z) * 2.0 - 1.0);\n                const r2 = 1.0 - Math.abs(valNoise(x * 2.15 + 4.7, z * 2.15 - 3.2) * 2.0 - 1.0);\n                const r3 = 1.0 - Math.abs(valNoise(x * 4.4 - 8.1, z * 4.4 + 6.5) * 2.0 - 1.0);\n                return (r1 * r1) * 0.56 + (r2 * r2) * 0.30 + r3 * 0.14;\n            };\n\n            // Specijalizovani GLSL šejder za alpske planine (oštre plavo-sive stenovite pukotine, beli snežni grebeni i meka magla u podnožju)\n            const mountainShaderMat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({\n                side: THREE.DoubleSide,\n                fog: false,\n                vertexShader: `\n                    varying vec3 vWorldPos;\n                    varying vec3 vNormal;\n                    void main() {\n                        vec4 wp = modelMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);\n                        vWorldPos = wp.xyz;\n                        vNormal = normalize(mat3(modelMatrix) * normal);\n                        gl_Position = projectionMatrix * viewMatrix * wp;\n                    }\n                `,\n                fragmentShader: `\n                    varying vec3 vWorldPos;\n                    varying vec3 vNormal;\n                    float hash21(vec2 p){return fract(sin(dot(p,vec2(127.1,311.7)))*43758.5453123);}\n                    float noise21(vec2 p){vec2 i=floor(p),f=fract(p);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);return mix(mix(hash21(i),hash21(i+vec2(1,0)),f.x),mix(hash21(i+vec2(0,1)),hash21(i+vec2(1,1)),f.x),f.y);}\n                    float fBm(vec2 p){return noise21(p)*.54+noise21(p*2.1)*.28+noise21(p*4.3)*.18;}\n                    void main() {\n                        vec2 mUV = vWorldPos.xy * 0.055 + vec2(vWorldPos.z * 0.035);\n                        float crag1 = 1.0 - abs(noise21(mUV * 2.2) * 2.0 - 1.0);\n                        float crag2 = 1.0 - abs(noise21(mUV * 5.4 + vec2(crag1 * 0.6)) * 2.0 - 1.0);\n                        float strata = fBm(vec2(vWorldPos.x * 0.04, vWorldPos.y * 0.11));\n\n                        vec3 n = normalize(vNormal + vec3((crag2 - 0.5) * 0.38, (strata - 0.5) * 0.22, (crag1 - 0.5) * 0.32));\n                        vec3 sunDir = normalize(vec3(-0.78, 0.46, 0.35));\n                        float sunLight = clamp(dot(n, sunDir) * 0.85 + 0.25, 0.0, 1.0);\n\n                        float slope = 1.0 - clamp(n.y, 0.0, 1.0);\n                        float couloir = smoothstep(0.35, 0.66, slope + (1.0 - crag1) * 0.32 - strata * 0.18);\n\n                        vec3 sunlitSnow = vec3(0.96, 0.985, 1.00);\n                        vec3 shadowSnow = vec3(0.58, 0.77, 0.94);\n                        vec3 darkRock   = vec3(0.32, 0.52, 0.76);\n                        vec3 lightRock  = vec3(0.52, 0.71, 0.89);\n\n                        vec3 snowCol = mix(shadowSnow, sunlitSnow, smoothstep(0.18, 0.78, sunLight));\n                        vec3 rockCol = mix(darkRock, lightRock, smoothstep(0.20, 0.82, sunLight * 0.7 + crag2 * 0.3));\n                        vec3 col = mix(snowCol, rockCol, couloir * 0.80);\n\n                        // Izmaglica i oblaci oko podnožja planinskog masiva\n                        float mistNoise = fBm(vec2(vWorldPos.x * 0.025, vWorldPos.y * 0.045));\n                        float baseMist = smoothstep(42.0, 8.0, vWorldPos.y - mistNoise * 12.0);\n                        vec3 mistCol = vec3(0.80, 0.91, 0.99);\n                        col = mix(col, mistCol, baseMist * 0.68);\n\n                        gl_FragColor = vec4(col, 1.0);\n                    }\n                `\n            });\n\n            function createAlpineRangeMesh(width, depth, segX, segZ, peaksConfig) {\n                const geo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(width, depth, segX, segZ);\n                geo.rotateX(-PI / 2);\n                const pos = geo.attributes.position;\n                const vCount = pos.count;\n\n                for (let i = 0; i < vCount; i++) {\n                    const vx = pos.getX(i);\n                    const vz = pos.getZ(i);\n                    const depthNorm = 1.0 - Math.abs(vz / (depth * 0.5));\n                    const edgeFade = Math.pow(Math.max(0, depthNorm), 0.70);\n\n                    let peakMax = 0;\n                    let shoulderSum = 0;\n                    for (let p = 0; p < peaksConfig.length; p++) {\n                        const [px, pz, pRad, pHeight, sharpness] = peaksConfig[p];\n                        const dx = (vx - px) / pRad;\n                        const dz = (vz - pz) / (pRad * 0.75);\n                        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dz);\n                        if (dist < 1.0) {\n                            const ridgeAngle = Math.atan2(vz - pz, vx - px);\n                            const arête = 1.0 + Math.cos(ridgeAngle * 3.0 + px * 0.04) * 0.18 + Math.sin(ridgeAngle * 5.0) * 0.08;\n                            const profile = Math.pow(1.0 - dist, sharpness) * arête;\n                            peakMax = Math.max(peakMax, pHeight * profile);\n                            shoulderSum += pHeight * profile * 0.14;\n                        }\n                    }\n\n                    const rn = ridgeNoise(vx * 0.022, vz * 0.022);\n                    const detail = ridgeNoise(vx * 0.062 + 11.3, vz * 0.062 - 7.4);\n                    const baseHills = (9.0 + rn * 15.0) * edgeFade;\n                    const finalY = Math.max(-1.5, ((peakMax + shoulderSum * 0.45) * (0.72 + rn * 0.28 + detail * 0.12) + baseHills) * edgeFade - 1.5);\n                    pos.setY(i, finalY);\n                }\n\n                geo.computeVertexNormals();\n                return new THREE.Mesh(geo, mountainShaderMat);\n            }\n\n            // 1) Glavni severni planinski masiv: dominantni piramidalni Matterhorn vrh u sredini (visina 108m, širina osnove 104m) i niži bočni grebeni levo i desno\n            const mainNorthPeaks = [\n                // Centralni široki piramidalni Matterhorn vrh (kao na referentnoj slici)\n                [14,   0, 104, 108, 1.38],\n                [-22,  6,  66,  64, 1.48],\n                [50,   4,  68,  72, 1.45],\n                // Leva strana: oštri vrh u daljini i nisko sedlo\n                [-72,  8,  66,  64, 1.42],\n                [-122, 12, 72,  48, 1.38],\n                [-172, 16, 78,  68, 1.35],\n                // Desna strana: bočni grebeni\n                [92,   6,  68,  66, 1.42],\n                [142, 10,  82,  78, 1.34],\n                [195, 14,  88,  72, 1.34]\n            ];\n            const northRange = createAlpineRangeMesh(520, 120, 220, 42, mainNorthPeaks);\n            northRange.position.set(8, 0, -195);\n            arcticVisualEnv.add(northRange);\n\n            // 2) Bočni planinski venci (zapad, istok, jug)\n            const sidePeaks = [\n                [-140, 0, 78, 68, 1.40],\n                [-65,  5, 68, 56, 1.45],\n                [10,  -4, 82, 78, 1.38],\n                [85,   6, 72, 62, 1.42],\n                [155,  0, 76, 70, 1.40]\n            ];\n            const westRange = createAlpineRangeMesh(440, 110, 120, 28, sidePeaks);\n            westRange.position.set(-200, 0, -10);\n            westRange.rotation.y = PI / 2;\n            arcticVisualEnv.add(westRange);\n\n            const eastRange = createAlpineRangeMesh(440, 110, 120, 28, sidePeaks);\n            eastRange.position.set(200, 0, -10);\n            eastRange.rotation.y = -PI / 2;\n            arcticVisualEnv.add(eastRange);\n\n            const southRange = createAlpineRangeMesh(460, 110, 120, 28, sidePeaks);\n            southRange.position.set(0, 0, 195);\n            southRange.rotation.y = PI;\n            arcticVisualEnv.add(southRange);\n\n            // 3) Meka izmaglica u podnožju planina\n            const mistPlaneGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(180, 38);\n            const mistMat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({\n                transparent: true,\n                depthWrite: false,\n                fog: false,\n                side: THREE.DoubleSide,\n                vertexShader: `varying vec2 vUv;void main(){vUv=uv;gl_Position=projectionMatrix*modelViewMatrix*vec4(position,1.0);}`,\n                fragmentShader: `\n                    varying vec2 vUv;\n                    void main(){\n                        vec2 c = vUv - vec2(0.5);\n                        float rx = smoothstep(0.5, 0.12, abs(c.x));\n                        float ry = smoothstep(0.5, 0.10, abs(c.y));\n                        float billow = 0.55 + 0.45 * sin(vUv.x * 9.0) * cos(vUv.y * 5.0);\n                        float alpha = rx * ry * billow * 0.48;\n                        gl_FragColor = vec4(0.86, 0.94, 1.0, alpha);\n                    }\n                `\n            });\n            for (let m = 0; m < 12; m++) {\n                const mist = new THREE.Mesh(mistPlaneGeo, mistMat);\n                const ang = (m / 12) * PI * 2;\n                const dist = 148 + (m % 3) * 16;\n                mist.position.set(Math.sin(ang) * dist, 18 + (m % 3) * 5, -Math.cos(ang) * dist);\n                mist.lookAt(0, 16, 0);\n                arcticVisualEnv.add(mist);\n            }\n\n            // 4) Ledene formacije na dalekom obodu ostrva\n            const iceMat = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({\n                color: 0x9cd4f8,\n                metalness: 0.06,\n                roughness: 0.25,\n                transparent: true,\n                opacity: 0.84,\n                clearcoat: 0.4,\n                clearcoatRoughness: 0.2\n            });\n            const shardGeo = new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.75, 2.8, 6, 1), shardCount = 60;\n            arcticIceShards = new THREE.InstancedMesh(shardGeo, iceMat, shardCount);\n            arcticIceShards.frustumCulled = false;\n            for (let i = 0; i < shardCount; i++) {\n                const angle = (i / shardCount) * Math.PI * 2 + rnd() * 0.13, rad = 82 + rnd() * 44;\n                const sx = Math.sin(angle) * rad, sz = Math.cos(angle) * rad;\n                if (sz < -10 && Math.abs(sx) < 65) {\n                    d.scale.set(0.001, 0.001, 0.001);\n                    d.updateMatrix();\n                    arcticIceShards.setMatrixAt(i, d.matrix);\n                    continue;\n                }\n                d.position.set(sx, getTerrainHeight(sx, sz) + 0.8 + 1.0 * rnd(), sz);\n                d.rotation.set((rnd() - 0.5) * 0.30, rnd() * 6.28, (rnd() - 0.5) * 0.28);\n                const size = 0.55 + rnd() * 1.2;\n                d.scale.set(size, size * (1.1 + rnd() * 1.6), size * 0.75);\n                d.updateMatrix();\n                arcticIceShards.setMatrixAt(i, d.matrix);\n            }\n            arcticIceShards.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;\n            arcticVisualEnv.add(arcticIceShards);\n\n            // 5) Niski snežni nanosi samo na dalekom obodu ostrva\n            const snowMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xf4f9ff, roughness: 0.72, metalness: 0.02 });\n            const driftGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(1, 12, 8), drifts = 80;\n            arcticSnowdrifts = new THREE.InstancedMesh(driftGeo, snowMat, drifts);\n            arcticSnowdrifts.frustumCulled = false;\n            for (let i = 0; i < drifts; i++) {\n                const a = rnd() * Math.PI * 2, r = 54 + rnd() * 55, x = Math.cos(a) * r, z = Math.sin(a) * r;\n                if (z < -5 && Math.abs(x) < 55) {\n                    d.scale.set(0.001, 0.001, 0.001);\n                } else {\n                    d.scale.set(2.5 + rnd() * 3.5, 0.14 + rnd() * 0.22, 2.2 + rnd() * 3.5);\n                }\n                d.position.set(x, getTerrainHeight(x, z) - 0.12, z);\n                d.rotation.set(0, a, 0);\n                d.updateMatrix();\n                arcticSnowdrifts.setMatrixAt(i, d.matrix);\n            }\n            arcticSnowdrifts.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;\n            arcticVisualEnv.add(arcticSnowdrifts);\n        }\n\n        function buildForestAndRocks() {\n            // 265 bogatih, organskih borova sa custom GLSL šejderom za zasićene tamnozelene četinarske iglice i meke zaobljene snežne jastučiće na granama!\n            const barkMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x3b281c, roughness: 0.92, metalness: 0.02 });\n\n            // GLSL šejder za zelene grane bora: bogata, zasićena tamnozelena boja četinara (bez bledila!)\n            const pineFoliageMat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({\n                vertexShader: `\n                    varying vec3 vNormal;\n                    varying vec3 vLocalPos;\n                    varying vec3 vWorldPos;\n                    void main() {\n                        vLocalPos = position;\n                        vec4 wp = modelMatrix * instanceMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);\n                        vWorldPos = wp.xyz;\n                        vNormal = normalize(mat3(modelMatrix) * mat3(instanceMatrix) * normal);\n                        gl_Position = projectionMatrix * viewMatrix * wp;\n                    }\n                `,\n                fragmentShader: `\n                    varying vec3 vNormal;\n                    varying vec3 vLocalPos;\n                    varying vec3 vWorldPos;\n                    float hash21(vec2 p){return fract(sin(dot(p,vec2(127.1,311.7)))*43758.5453123);}\n                    float noise21(vec2 p){vec2 i=floor(p),f=fract(p);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);return mix(mix(hash21(i),hash21(i+vec2(1,0)),f.x),mix(hash21(i+vec2(0,1)),hash21(i+vec2(1,1)),f.x),f.y);}\n                    void main() {\n                        vec3 n = normalize(vNormal);\n                        vec3 sunDir = normalize(vec3(-0.82, 0.42, 0.25));\n                        float sunDot = clamp(dot(n, sunDir) * 0.78 + 0.24, 0.0, 1.0);\n\n                        float needleNoise = noise21(vWorldPos.xy * 6.5 + vWorldPos.z * 4.5);\n                        float clumpNoise  = noise21(vWorldPos.xz * 2.8 + vec2(vLocalPos.y * 4.5));\n\n                        // Zasićena, prirodna četinarska zelena (tamna šumska u senci, bogata borova zelena na suncu)\n                        vec3 darkPine  = vec3(0.03, 0.17, 0.06);\n                        vec3 midPine   = vec3(0.08, 0.36, 0.12);\n                        vec3 sunnyPine = vec3(0.20, 0.50, 0.15);\n                        vec3 pineCol = mix(darkPine, midPine, smoothstep(0.08, 0.52, sunDot + needleNoise * 0.16));\n                        pineCol = mix(pineCol, sunnyPine, smoothstep(0.50, 0.90, sunDot + clumpNoise * 0.20));\n\n                        // Bogati beli snežni jastučići na gornjoj strani grana (dok su donji obodi grana čisto tamnozeleni!)\n                        float snowLobe = sin(atan(vLocalPos.z, vLocalPos.x) * 6.0 + vWorldPos.y * 1.5) * 0.14;\n                        float snowMask = smoothstep(-0.08, 0.18, vLocalPos.y + snowLobe + (clumpNoise - 0.5) * 0.22) * smoothstep(0.10, 0.42, n.y);\n                        vec3 snowShadow = vec3(0.55, 0.75, 0.93);\n                        vec3 snowBright = vec3(0.97, 0.99, 1.00);\n                        vec3 snowCol = mix(snowShadow, snowBright, smoothstep(0.20, 0.72, sunDot));\n                        vec3 col = mix(pineCol, snowCol, snowMask * 0.92);\n\n                        float dist = distance(cameraPosition, vWorldPos);\n                        float fog = clamp(1.0 - exp(-dist * 0.0005), 0.0, 0.42);\n                        col = mix(col, vec3(0.76, 0.89, 0.99), fog);\n                        gl_FragColor = vec4(col, 1.0);\n                    }\n                `\n            });\n\n            // GLSL šejder za zaobljene snežne jastučiće na granama\n            const pineSnowMat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({\n                vertexShader: `\n                    varying vec3 vNormal;\n                    varying vec3 vLocalPos;\n                    varying vec3 vWorldPos;\n                    void main() {\n                        vLocalPos = position;\n                        vec4 wp = modelMatrix * instanceMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);\n                        vWorldPos = wp.xyz;\n                        vNormal = normalize(mat3(modelMatrix) * mat3(instanceMatrix) * normal);\n                        gl_Position = projectionMatrix * viewMatrix * wp;\n                    }\n                `,\n                fragmentShader: `\n                    varying vec3 vNormal;\n                    varying vec3 vLocalPos;\n                    varying vec3 vWorldPos;\n                    float hash21(vec2 p){return fract(sin(dot(p,vec2(127.1,311.7)))*43758.5453123);}\n                    float noise21(vec2 p){vec2 i=floor(p),f=fract(p);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);return mix(mix(hash21(i),hash21(i+vec2(1,0)),f.x),mix(hash21(i+vec2(0,1)),hash21(i+vec2(1,1)),f.x),f.y);}\n                    void main() {\n                        float puff = noise21(vWorldPos.xy * 3.5 + vWorldPos.z * 2.8);\n                        vec3 n = normalize(vNormal + vec3((puff - 0.5) * 0.25, 0.15, (puff - 0.5) * 0.25));\n                        vec3 sunDir = normalize(vec3(-0.82, 0.42, 0.25));\n                        float sunDot = clamp(dot(n, sunDir) * 0.80 + 0.26, 0.0, 1.0);\n\n                        // Na donjoj ivici snežnog jastučića proviruju tamnozelene iglice bora!\n                        float greenRim = smoothstep(-0.08, -0.42, vLocalPos.y + (puff - 0.5) * 0.32);\n                        vec3 snowShadow = vec3(0.54, 0.74, 0.92);\n                        vec3 snowBright = vec3(0.97, 0.99, 1.00);\n                        vec3 snowCol = mix(snowShadow, snowBright, smoothstep(0.20, 0.75, sunDot));\n                        vec3 pineCol = mix(vec3(0.04, 0.20, 0.07), vec3(0.16, 0.44, 0.14), sunDot);\n                        vec3 col = mix(snowCol, pineCol, greenRim * 0.92);\n\n                        float dist = distance(cameraPosition, vWorldPos);\n                        float fog = clamp(1.0 - exp(-dist * 0.0005), 0.0, 0.42);\n                        col = mix(col, vec3(0.78, 0.90, 0.99), fog);\n                        gl_FragColor = vec4(col, 1.0);\n                    }\n                `\n            });\n\n            const moundSnowMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xf0f7ff, roughness: 0.72, metalness: 0.02 });\n\n            const totalTrees = 265;\n            const tiersPerTree = 7;\n\n            // Visokodetaljni, glatko zaobljeni konus grana (16 segmenata)\n            const coneGeo = new THREE.ConeGeometry(1.0, 1.0, 16, 4);\n            const cPos = coneGeo.attributes.position;\n            for (let k = 0; k < cPos.count; k++) {\n                const px = cPos.getX(k), py = cPos.getY(k), pz = cPos.getZ(k);\n                const ang = Math.atan2(pz, px);\n                const rNorm = Math.hypot(px, pz);\n                if (rNorm > 0.01) {\n                    const boughWave = 1.0 + Math.sin(ang * 7.0 + py * 2.5) * 0.18 + Math.cos(ang * 4.0) * 0.09;\n                    const droop = (py < 0.0) ? -Math.pow(rNorm, 1.4) * 0.15 * (0.6 + 0.4 * Math.sin(ang * 7.0)) : 0.0;\n                    cPos.setX(k, px * boughWave);\n                    cPos.setY(k, py + droop);\n                    cPos.setZ(k, pz * boughWave);\n                }\n            }\n            coneGeo.computeVertexNormals();\n\n            // Zaobljeni snežni jastučić za gornju stranu grana\n            const snowCapGeo = new THREE.ConeGeometry(1.0, 1.0, 16, 4);\n            const sPos = snowCapGeo.attributes.position;\n            for (let k = 0; k < sPos.count; k++) {\n                const px = sPos.getX(k), py = sPos.getY(k), pz = sPos.getZ(k);\n                const ang = Math.atan2(pz, px);\n                const rNorm = Math.hypot(px, pz);\n                if (rNorm > 0.01) {\n                    const pillowWave = 0.80 + Math.cos(ang * 7.0 + 0.4) * 0.20 + Math.sin(ang * 3.0) * 0.08;\n                    const curveY = py + Math.sin((1.0 - rNorm) * PI) * 0.14;\n                    sPos.setX(k, px * pillowWave);\n                    sPos.setY(k, curveY);\n                    sPos.setZ(k, pz * pillowWave);\n                }\n            }\n            snowCapGeo.computeVertexNormals();\n\n            const trunkBaseGeo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.12, 0.30, 1.0, 7);\n            const trunkInst = new THREE.InstancedMesh(trunkBaseGeo, barkMat, totalTrees);\n            pineNeedlesInst = new THREE.InstancedMesh(coneGeo, pineFoliageMat, totalTrees * tiersPerTree);\n            pineSnowCapInst = new THREE.InstancedMesh(snowCapGeo, pineSnowMat, totalTrees * tiersPerTree);\n            snowMoundInst = new THREE.InstancedMesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(1.0, 10, 8), moundSnowMat, totalTrees + 20);\n\n            trunkInst.castShadow = true; trunkInst.receiveShadow = true;\n            pineNeedlesInst.castShadow = true; pineNeedlesInst.receiveShadow = true;\n            pineSnowCapInst.castShadow = true; pineSnowCapInst.receiveShadow = true;\n            snowMoundInst.receiveShadow = true;\n            trunkInst.frustumCulled = false;\n            pineNeedlesInst.frustumCulled = false;\n            pineSnowCapInst.frustumCulled = false;\n            snowMoundInst.frustumCulled = false;\n\n            const dummy = new THREE.Object3D();\n            let foliageIdx = 0, snowCapIdx = 0, moundIdx = 0;\n\n            const curatedTrees = [\n                // Leva prednja grupa borova (odmah na levoj ivici kadra, baca duge plave senke preko snega)\n                [-4.2, -8.5, 9.8], [-5.5, -10.6, 8.2], [-3.2, -11.8, 6.0], [-6.8, -12.8, 10.8], [-4.8, -14.8, 7.2],\n                [-8.4, -10.8, 9.2], [-10.2, -13.8, 11.4], [-6.4, -17.2, 6.5], [-11.8, -16.5, 9.5],\n                // Srednji šumski venac duž snežnog grebena (z = -28 do -36, raznovrsne visine 4.2m - 8.6m)\n                [-8.5, -28.0, 5.2], [-5.2, -29.5, 7.2], [-2.0, -27.8, 4.6], [1.2, -29.0, 6.8], [4.2, -27.2, 5.0],\n                [7.4, -28.5, 7.8], [10.5, -26.8, 5.6], [13.8, -28.0, 8.2], [16.8, -26.2, 5.4], [20.0, -27.5, 8.6],\n                [23.2, -25.8, 6.0], [26.5, -27.0, 8.0], [29.8, -25.2, 6.6], [33.0, -26.5, 8.8], [36.2, -24.8, 6.2],\n                [39.5, -26.0, 8.4], [42.8, -24.2, 7.0], [46.0, -25.5, 9.0],\n                // Drugi red šume iza grebena\n                [-10.2, -32.5, 7.8], [-6.8, -34.0, 6.0], [-3.4, -32.0, 8.4], [0.0, -33.5, 5.5], [3.2, -31.8, 8.0],\n                [6.5, -33.2, 6.4], [9.8, -31.2, 8.6], [13.2, -32.8, 5.8], [16.5, -31.0, 7.8], [19.8, -32.5, 7.0],\n                [23.2, -30.5, 8.8], [26.6, -32.0, 6.4], [30.0, -30.0, 8.2], [33.5, -31.5, 6.2], [37.0, -29.5, 8.6],\n                [40.5, -31.0, 7.2], [44.2, -29.0, 9.2],\n                // Leva pozadinska linija šume\n                [-16.5, -28.5, 6.8], [-20.0, -31.0, 8.4], [-23.8, -27.8, 5.5], [-27.5, -30.2, 7.8], [-31.2, -27.2, 6.2],\n                [-35.0, -29.5, 8.6], [-39.0, -26.5, 5.8]\n            ];\n\n            let seedTree = 94821;\n            const trnd = () => ((seedTree = (seedTree * 1664525 + 1013904223) >>> 0) / 4294967296);\n\n            for (let i = 0; i < totalTrees;) {\n                let tx, tz, h, isOuter;\n                if (i < curatedTrees.length) {\n                    tx = curatedTrees[i][0];\n                    tz = curatedTrees[i][1];\n                    h = curatedTrees[i][2];\n                    isOuter = false;\n                    i++;\n                } else {\n                    isOuter = i >= 130;\n                    const clusterAng = ((i * 0.6180339) % 1.0) * PI * 2;\n                    const clusterDist = isOuter ? (46 + trnd() * 78) : (18 + trnd() * 52);\n                    const jitterX = (trnd() - 0.5) * 14.0;\n                    const jitterZ = (trnd() - 0.5) * 14.0;\n                    tx = Math.cos(clusterAng) * clusterDist + jitterX;\n                    tz = Math.sin(clusterAng) * clusterDist + jitterZ;\n\n                    if (Math.hypot(tx, tz + 2) < 13.5) continue;\n                    if (Math.hypot(tx + 7.5, tz + 7.5) < 11.2) continue;\n                    if (Math.hypot(tx - 28, tz + 32) < 9.2) continue;\n                    if (Math.abs(tx + 7.5) < 7.5 && tz < -3 && tz > -68) continue;\n                    if (tx > -3.2 && tx < 42.0 && tz < 2.0 && tz > -25.0) continue;\n\n                    const sizeRoll = trnd();\n                    h = sizeRoll < 0.34 ? (4.2 + trnd() * 2.0) : (sizeRoll < 0.78 ? (6.2 + trnd() * 2.4) : (8.6 + trnd() * 3.0));\n                    i++;\n                }\n\n                const ty = getTerrainHeight(tx, tz);\n                const leanX = (trnd() - 0.5) * 0.035;\n                const leanZ = (trnd() - 0.5) * 0.035;\n\n                // Stablo bora\n                dummy.position.set(tx, ty + h * 0.35, tz);\n                dummy.rotation.set(leanX, (i * 1.37) % (PI * 2), leanZ);\n                dummy.scale.set(0.85, h * 0.70, 0.85);\n                dummy.updateMatrix();\n                trunkInst.setMatrixAt(i - 1, dummy.matrix);\n\n                // Blag snežni nanos uz samo stablo\n                dummy.position.set(tx, ty + 0.02, tz);\n                dummy.rotation.set(0, i * 0.8, 0);\n                const moundR = (0.72 + (i % 3) * 0.14) * (h / 10.0);\n                dummy.scale.set(moundR, 0.16, moundR * 1.05);\n                dummy.updateMatrix();\n                snowMoundInst.setMatrixAt(moundIdx++, dummy.matrix);\n\n                // 7 gustih slojeva četinarske krošnje: tamnozelene grane jasno proviruju ispod belih snežnih jastučića!\n                const widthScale = (0.68 + (i % 5) * 0.04) * (h / 9.4);\n                for (let tier = 0; tier < tiersPerTree; tier++) {\n                    const tFrac = tier / (tiersPerTree - 1);\n                    const tierY = ty + h * (0.15 + tFrac * 0.74);\n                    const radius = (2.35 - tFrac * 1.82) * widthScale;\n                    const coneH = (2.15 - tFrac * 0.85) * (h / 9.2);\n\n                    // Bogate tamnozelene grane bora\n                    dummy.position.set(tx + leanX * tierY * 0.20, tierY, tz + leanZ * tierY * 0.20);\n                    dummy.rotation.set(leanX, i * 0.95 + tier * 1.15, leanZ);\n                    dummy.scale.set(radius * 1.12, coneH, radius * 1.12);\n                    dummy.updateMatrix();\n                    pineNeedlesInst.setMatrixAt(foliageIdx++, dummy.matrix);\n\n                    // Zaobljeni beli snežni jastučići na gornjim granama (tamnozelene grane vire ispod svakog sloja!)\n                    dummy.position.set(tx + leanX * tierY * 0.20 - 0.05, tierY + coneH * 0.17, tz + leanZ * tierY * 0.20);\n                    dummy.rotation.set(leanX, i * 0.95 + tier * 1.15 + 0.35, leanZ);\n                    dummy.scale.set(radius * 0.96, coneH * 0.62, radius * 0.96);\n                    dummy.updateMatrix();\n                    pineSnowCapInst.setMatrixAt(snowCapIdx++, dummy.matrix);\n                }\n\n                if (!isOuter) {\n                    treeColliders.push({ x: tx, z: tz, r: 0.65 });\n                }\n            }\n\n            while (moundIdx < totalTrees + 20) {\n                dummy.scale.set(0.001, 0.001, 0.001);\n                dummy.updateMatrix();\n                snowMoundInst.setMatrixAt(moundIdx++, dummy.matrix);\n            }\n\n            trunkInst.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;\n            pineNeedlesInst.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;\n            pineSnowCapInst.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;\n            snowMoundInst.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;\n            scene.add(trunkInst, pineNeedlesInst, pineSnowCapInst, snowMoundInst);\n\n            // 24 slojevite arktičke stene na obodu ostrva\n            const rockGeo = new THREE.DodecahedronGeometry(2.8, 1);\n            const rPos = rockGeo.attributes.position;\n            for (let j = 0; j < rPos.count; j++) {\n                rPos.setY(j, rPos.getY(j) * 0.78);\n            }\n            rockGeo.computeVertexNormals();\n            const cliffMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x5c82ab, roughness: 0.82, metalness: 0.05, flatShading: true });\n\n            for (let r = 0; r < 24; r++) {\n                const a = (r / 24) * PI * 2;\n                if (Math.sin(a) < -0.25) continue;\n                const dist = (r < 18) ? (66 + trnd() * 22) : (32 + trnd() * 28);\n                const rx = Math.cos(a) * dist;\n                const rz = Math.sin(a) * dist;\n                if (Math.hypot(rx, rz + 2) < 16 || Math.hypot(rx + 7.5, rz + 7.5) < 14) continue;\n\n                const rockGroup = new THREE.Group();\n                const rock = new THREE.Mesh(rockGeo, r % 2 === 0 ? floorMat : cliffMat);\n                rock.castShadow = true; rock.receiveShadow = true;\n                const sx = 1.0 + trnd() * 1.35, sy = 0.70 + trnd() * 1.15, sz = 1.0 + trnd() * 1.20;\n                rock.scale.set(sx, sy, sz);\n                rock.rotation.set(r * 0.4, r * 0.7, 0);\n                rockGroup.add(rock);\n\n                const rockSnow = new THREE.Mesh(rockGeo, moundSnowMat);\n                rockSnow.scale.set(sx * 0.94, sy * 0.50, sz * 0.94);\n                rockSnow.position.y = sy * 0.95;\n                rockSnow.rotation.copy(rock.rotation);\n                rockGroup.add(rockSnow);\n\n                rockGroup.position.set(rx, getTerrainHeight(rx, rz) + 0.7, rz);\n                scene.add(rockGroup);\n            }\n        }\n\n        function buildBlizzardParticles() {\n            const snowGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();\n            const posArr = new Float32Array(SNOW_COUNT * 3);\n            for (let i = 0; i < SNOW_COUNT; i++) {\n                posArr[i * 3]     = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 110;\n                posArr[i * 3 + 1] = Math.random() * 26;\n                posArr[i * 3 + 2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 110;\n                snowVelocities.push([\n                    (Math.random() - 0.35) * 0.09,\n                    -(Math.random() * 0.14 + 0.07),\n                    (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.07\n                ]);\n            }\n            snowGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(posArr, 3));\n            const flakeCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');\n            flakeCanvas.width = 32; flakeCanvas.height = 32;\n            const fCtx = flakeCanvas.getContext('2d');\n            const grad = fCtx.createRadialGradient(16, 16, 0, 16, 16, 15);\n            grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.95)');\n            grad.addColorStop(0.5, 'rgba(225,242,255,0.45)');\n            grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(225,242,255,0)');\n            fCtx.fillStyle = grad;\n            fCtx.fillRect(0, 0, 32, 32);\n            const flakeTex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(flakeCanvas);\n            const snowMat = new THREE.PointsMaterial({\n                color: 0xffffff,\n                map: flakeTex,\n                size: 0.14,\n                transparent: true,\n                opacity: 0.18,\n                depthWrite: false\n            });\n            snowParticles = new THREE.Points(snowGeo, snowMat);\n            snowParticles.frustumCulled = false;\n            scene.add(snowParticles);\n        }\n\n        function buildRepairableAircraft() {\n            planeGroup = new THREE.Group();\n\n            const metalMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x8294a5, roughness: 0.35, metalness: 0.68 });\n            const redStripeMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x9b2731, roughness: 0.46, metalness: .27 });\n            const darkMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x0f172a, roughness: 0.85 });\n\n            // Glavni trup aviona\n            const fuselage = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.8, 1.18, 9.2, 14), metalMat);\n            fuselage.rotation.x = PI / 2;\n            fuselage.position.y = 1.35;\n            planeGroup.add(fuselage);\n\n            // Crvena arktička šara na trupu\n            const stripe = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.95, 1.08,           const noseCone = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(1.18, 2.5, 14), redStripeMat);\n            noseCone.rotation.x = -PI / 2;\n            noseCone.position.set(0, 1.35, -5.85);\n            planeGroup.add(noseCone);\n\n            // Arktičke skije za sletanje na led (Landing Skis)\n            const skiGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.32, 0.1, 3.8);\n            const strutGeo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.06, 0.06, 0.95, 6);\n            [-1.05, 1.05].forEach((sx) => {\n                const ski = new THREE.Mesh(skiGeo, darkMat);\n                ski.position.set(sx, 0.12,            leftWingGroup = new THREE.Group();\n            leftWingGroup.position.set(-1.0, 1.2, -0.4);\n            const lWingMesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(5.6, 0.18, 2.5), }));\n            lNavLight.position.set(-5.4, 0.12, 0);\n            leftWingGroup.add(lWingMesh, lNavLight);\n\n            rightWingGroup = new THREE.Group();\n            rightWingGroup.position.set(1.0, 1.2, -0.4);\n            const rWingMesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(5.6, 0.18, 2.5), metalMat);\n            rWingMesh.position.x = 2.7;\n            const rightWingGroup.add(rWingMesh, rNavLight);\n\n            planeGroup.add(leftWingGroup, rightWingGroup);\n\n            // Repna sekcija na avionu\n            const vStabilizer = new THREE.Mesh(new canopyMesh = new THREE.Mesh(\n                new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.55, 0.82, 2.5),\n                new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x7dd3fc, transparent: true, opacity: 0.58, roughness:            cockpitFrameGroup.visible = false;\n            planeGroup.add(cockpitFrameGroup);\n\n            // Putnička kabina, instrumentacija, sedišta i posada sa pilotskim kacigama.\n            cabinCrewGroup = new THREE.Group();\n            cabinCrewGroup.visible = false;\n            planeGroup.add(cabinCrewGroup);\n            buildAircraftInterior();\n\n            // 3-kraki propeler na nosu (skriven dok se ne ugradi turbina)\n            propellerGroup = new THREE.Group();\n            propellerGroup.position.set(0, 1.35, -7.1);\n            for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {\n                const bladeHolder = new THREE.Group();\n                bladeHolder.rotation.z = (i * PI * 2) / 3;\n                const blade = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.28, 2.45, 0.08), metalMat);\n                blade.position.y = 1.22;\n                bladeHolder.add(blade);\n                propellerGroup.add(bladeHolder);\n            }\n            const spinner = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.32, 10, 10), redStripeMat);\n            propellerGroup.add(spinner);\n            propellerGroup.visible = false;\n            planeGroup.add(propellerGroup);\n\n            addAircraftExteriorDetail(planeGroup,metalMat,redStripeMat,darkMat);\n            planeGroup.position.copy(PLANE_START_POS);\n            planeGroup.rotation.y = 0.15; // Usmeren ka severnom koridoru\n            scene.add(planeGroup);\n            treeColliders.push({ x: PLANE_START_POS.x, z: PLANE_START_POS.z, r: 5.2 });\n\n            planeStrobeLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xff2222, 2.6, 24);\n            planeStrobeLight.position.set(PLANE_START_POS.x, 3.8, PLANE_START_POS.z);\n            scene.add(planeStrobeLight);\n\n            // Odlomljena repna sekcija duboko u šumi (28, 0, -32) pored koje je turbina\n            const brokenTail = new THREE.Group();\n            const tCone = new THREE.Mesh(new props=group.userData.props,badge=group.userData.badgeChar;\n            for(const key of ['phone','flashlight','wrench','toolbox','tablet','rope'])if(props[key])props[key].visible=false;\n    props.wrench.visible=mode!=='seat';if(props.leftToolbox)props.leftToolbox.visible=mode!=='seat';\n            } else if(badge==='P')props.tablet.visible=mode!=='seat';\n        }\n        function animateCharacter(charGroup,time,state='idle'){\n            if(!charGroup||!charGroup.userData.rig)return;\n            const r=charGroup.userData.rig,s=Math.sin,c=Math.cos;\n            const walking=(state==='walk'||state==='run');\n            const running=(state==='run');\n            const interacting=(state==='interact');\n            const sitting=['sit','pilot','pilotPanic','brace'].includes(state);\n            const talking=['talk','happy','panic','pilotTalk','pilotPanic','brace'].includes(state)||charGroup.userData.speaking===true;\n            const panic=['panic','brace','pilotPanic'].includes(state);\n            const cold=state==='cold';\n            const wave=state==='wave';\n\n            const freq=running?11.6:7.8;\n            const strideAmp=running?0.92:(walking?0.64:0);\n            const gait=walking?s(time*freq):0;\n            const gaitCos=walking?c(time*freq):0;\n\n            // Prirodno disanje grudnog koša i nagib pri trčanju kroz dubok sneg\n            r.torso.scale.set(1+s(time*2.2)*.012, 1+s(time*2.2)*.022, 1+s(time*2.2)*.016);\n            updatePremiumCharacterMotion(charGroup,time,state);\n            if(walking && !sitting){\n                r.torso.position.y = 0.95 + Math.abs(gaitCos)*0.048;\n                r.head.position.y = 1.62 + Math.abs(gaitCos)*0.044;\n            }\n            r.torso.rotation.x = sitting ? 0 : (running ? 0.19 : (walking ? 0.08 : (interacting ? 0.35 : 0)));\n            r.torso.rotation.y = walking ? -gait*0.11 : 0;\n            r.torso.rotation.z = (cold?s(time*27)*.042:0)+(panic?s(time*18)*.045:0)+(walking?gaitCos*0.035:0);\n\n            const customLookYaw = charGroup.userData.lookYaw || 0;\n            const customLookPitch = charGroup.userData.lookPitch || 0;\n            r.head.rotation.y = (talking?s(time*1.65)*.11:s(time*0.7+charGroup.id)*.05) + (wave?-.2:0) + customLookYaw;\n            r.head.rotation.x = panic?.09+s(time*7)*.075 : (talking?s(time*2.3)*.075 : (interacting?0.25:customLookPitch));\n            r.jaw.position.y = -.109-(talking?(Math.abs(s(time*13.5))* .046):0);\n\n            r.brows.forEach((b,i)=>{b.rotation.z=(i===0?-1:1)*(panic?-.28:state==='happy'?.22:cold?-.16:running?-.12:0);});\n            r.eyes.forEach((eye,i)=>{eye.position.x=(i?1:-1)*.082+ s(time*1.1)*.008;});\n\n            // Zglobovi kukova i kolena sa prirodnom savijanjem pri koraku\n            r.leftHip.rotation.x = sitting ? -1.25 : gait*strideAmp;\n            r.rightHip.rotation.x = sitting ? -1.25 : -gait*strideAmp;\n            r.leftKnee.rotation.x = sitting ? 1.36 : walking ? Math.max(0.05, -gait)*(running?1.08:0.78) : (interacting?0.42:0.04);\n            const pickupBend=charGroup.userData.pickupProgress||0;\n            if(pickupBend){r.torso.rotation.x+=pickupBend*.48;r.head.rotation.x+=pickupBend*.18;r.leftKnee.rotation.x+=pickupBend*.35;r.rightKnee.rotation.x+=pickupBend*.48;}\n            r.rightKnee.rotation.x = sitting ? 1.36 : walking ? Math.max(0.05, gait)*(running?1.08:0.78) : (interacting?0.42:0.04);\n\n            // Ruke i laktovi (Andrej drži lampu napred kad istražuje, Lazar drži alat, Petar skener)\n            const badge = charGroup.userData.badgeChar;\n            const holdingLamp = (badge==='A' && (state==='idle'||state==='walk'||state==='run') && missionState===STATE_SURVIVAL && flashlightOn);\n            r.leftShoulder.rotation.x = sitting ? -.72 : (interacting ? -0.95 : (walking ? -gait*(running?.72:.48) : talking ? -.38-s(time*4)*.18 : wave ? -.3 : -0.06));\n            r.rightShoulder.rotation.x = sitting ? -.72 : (interacting ? -1.05 : (holdingLamp ? -0.68+gait*0.14 : (walking ? gait*(running?.72:.48) : talking ? -.42+s(time*4+.4)*.18 : wave ? -1.7 : -0.12)));\n            r.leftShoulder.rotation.z = panic?-.35:talking?.20:cold?.12:walking?-0.08:-0.05;\n            r.rightShoulder.rotation.z = wave?-1.1:panic?.35:talking?-.18:cold?-.12:walking?0.08:0.05;\n            if(pickupBend)r.rightShoulder.rotation.x=-1.15-pickupBend*.65;\n            r.leftElbow.rotation.x = sitting?-1.00:interacting?-0.75:running?-0.82:walking?-0.38:talking?-.55:cold?-.45:-0.18;\n            r.rightElbow.rotation.x = sitting?-1.00:interacting?-0.85:holdingLamp?-0.62:running?-0.85:walking?-0.42:talking?-.65:state==='phone'?-1.30:-0.25;\n\n            if(state==='phone'){r.rightShoulder.rotation.x=-.92;r.rightElbow.rotation.x=-1.15;r.rightShoulder.rotation.z=-.20;}\n            if(state==='pilot'||state==='pilotPanic'){r.leftShoulder.rotation.x=-.88+(state==='pilotPanic'?s(time*11)*.2:0);r.rightShoulder.rotation.x=-.88+(state==='pilotPanic'?s(time*9)*.2:0);r.leftElbow.rotation.x=-.82;r.rightElbow.rotation.x=-.82;}\n            if(state==='brace'){r.leftShoulder.rotation.x=-1.1;r.rightShoulder.rotation.x=-1.1;r.head.rotation.x=.32;}\n\n            r.breath.visible=['cold','idle','walk','run'].includes(state) && (missionState===STATE_SURVIVAL||missionState===STATE_OUTRO_CUTSCENE);\n            const bf=(time*(running?.75:.45))%1;r.breath.position.z=.32+bf*.52;r.breath.position.y=-.14+bf*.16;r.breath.scale.setScalar(.65+bf*2.0);\n            r.breath.material.opacity=r.breath.visible?Math.max(0,.18*(1-bf)):0;\n            updateArcticFaceRig(charGroup,time,state);\n        }\n\n        function createAircraftSeat(parent,x,y,z,scale=1){\n premiumLastAct=-1;\n        function resetPremiumCinematic(){\n          premiumLastAct=-1;\n          if(premiumActLight)premiumActLight.intensity=2.1;\n          if(premiumCabinLight)premiumCabinLight.intensity=1.65;\n          if(premiumAshGlow)premiumAshGlow.intensity=2.2;\n          if(renderer)renderer.toneMappingExposure=0.94;\n          const    const act=t<7.5?0:t<14?1:t<22?2:3;\n          if(act!==premiumLastAct){\n            premiumLastAct=act;\n            const l=document.getElementById('cin-location');\n e=act===0?1.32:act===1?1.22:act===2?.98:1.01;\n          renderer.toneMappingExposure+=(e-renderer.toneMappingExposure)*Math.min(1,dt*2.0);\n KONTROLE, PROMENA LIKA, INTERAKCIJA [E] I POLETANJE\n        // ============================================================================\n        function setupControls() {\n            const container = $('canvas-container');\n            const requestLock = () => {\n                try {\n                    const p = container.requestPointerLock();\n                    if (p && p.catch) p.catch(() => {});\n                } catch (e) {}\n            };\n\n            const start3DSimulation = (skipIntro = false) => {\n                if (gameStarted) return;\n                initAudioEngine();\n                if (audioCtx && !eliteMuted && audioCtx.state === 'suspended') audioCtx.resume().catch(()=>{});\n                requestLock();\n                $('overlay').style.display = 'none';\n                gameStarted = true;\n                beginIntroCutscene();\n                if (skipIntro) {\n                    finishIntroCutscene();\n                }\n            };\n            window.arkStart3DNow = start3DSimulation;\n            $('start-btn').onclick = () => start3DSimulation(false);\n            $('cin-skip').onclick = skipCinematic;\n\n            $('restart-btn').onclick = () => window.location.reload();\n            $('btn-f').onclick = toggleFlashlight;\n            $('btn-e').onclick = handleInteraction;\n            if ($('btn-v')) {\n                $('btn-v').onclick = () => {\n                    thirdPersonMode = !thirdPersonMode;\n                    playSfx('click');\n                    setSubtitle(thirdPersonMode ? 'Kamera: 3. LICE ( glatko praćenje aktivnog lika )' : 'Kamera: 1. LICE ( direktan pogled )');\n                };\n            }\n            $('btn-c1').onclick = () => switchCharacter('petar');\n            $('btn-c2').onclick = () => switchCharacter('lazar');\n            $('btn-c3').onclick = () => switchCharacter('andrej');\n\n            window.addEventListener('wheel', (e) => {\n                if (!gameStarted || gameOver || gamePhase !== 'survival') return;\n                targetCamDist = Math.max(2.1, Math.min(7.2, targetCamDist + (e.deltaY > 0 ? 0.45 : -0.45)));\n            }, { passive: true });\n\n            document.addEventListener('pointerlockchange', () => {\n                isPointerLocked = (document.pointerLockElement === container);\n            });\n\n            container.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {\n                if (!gameStarted || gameOver || (gamePhase !== 'survival' && missionState!==STATE_PHASE3_READY)) return;\n                if (!isPointerLocked) requestLock();\n                isDraggingMouse = true;\n                prevMouseX = e.clientX;\n                prevMouseY = e.clientY;\n            });\n\n            window.addEventListener('mouseup', () => { isDraggingMouse = false; });\n\n            document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {\n                if (!gameStarted || gameOver || (gamePhase !== 'survival' && missionState!==STATE_PHASE3_READY)) return;\n                const sens = 0.0023 * eliteSensitivity;\n                if (isPointerLocked) {\n                    yaw -= e.movementX * sens;\n                    pitch -= e.movementY * sens;\n                } else if (isDraggingMouse) {\n                    yaw -= (e.clientX - prevMouseX) * sens * 1.4;\n                    pitch -= (e.clientY - prevMouseY) * sens * 1.4;\n                    prevMouseX = e.clientX;\n                    prevMouseY = e.clientY;\n                }\n                pitch = Math.max(-1.32, Math.min(1.32, pitch));\n            });\n\n            const handleMovementKeys = (code, isDown) => {\n                switch (code) {\n                    case 'KeyW': case 'ArrowUp': moveF = isDown; return true;\n                    case 'KeyS': case 'ArrowDown': moveB = isDown; return true;\n                    case 'KeyA': case 'ArrowLeft': moveL = isDown; return true;\n                    case 'KeyD': case 'ArrowRight': moveR = isDown; return true;\n                    case 'ShiftLeft': case 'ShiftRight': isRunning = isDown; return true;\n                    case 'Space': isBraking = isDown; return true;\n                    default: return false;\n                }\n            };\n\n            document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {\n                window._arkKeys[e.code]=true;\n                if (!gameStarted || gameOver || elitePaused) return;\n                if(missionState===STATE_FLIGHT && ark2.landed && e.code==='Space'){ark2FinishLanding();e.preventDefault();return;}\n                if (missionState === STATE_INTRO_CUTSCENE || missionState === STATE_TAKEOFF_CUTSCENE || missionState === STATE_OUTRO_CUTSCENE) {\n                    if (e.code === 'Space') { e.preventDefault(); skipCinematic(); }\n                    return;\n                }\n                if (handleMovementKeys(e.code, true)) {\n                    e.preventDefault();\n                    return;\n                }\n                switch (e.code) {\n                    case 'KeyF':\n                        if (gamePhase === 'survival') toggleFlashlight();\n                        break;\n                    case 'KeyE':\n                        handleInteraction();\n                        break;\n                    case 'KeyV':\n                        if (gamePhase === 'survival') {\n                            thirdPersonMode = !thirdPersonMode;\n                            playSfx('click');\n                            setSubtitle(thirdPersonMode ? 'Kamera: 3. LICE ( glatko praćenje aktivnog lika )' : 'Kamera: 1. LICE ( direktan pogled )');\n                        }\n                        break;\n                    case 'KeyC':\n                        if (gamePhase === 'flight') {\n                            cockpitView = !cockpitView;\n                            $('fl-cam').textContent = cockpitView ? 'KOKPIT (1. LICE)' : 'TREĆE LICE';\n                            playSfx('click');\n                        } else if (gamePhase === 'survival') {\n                            thirdPersonMode = !thirdPersonMode;\n                            playSfx('click');\n                            setSubtitle(thirdPersonMode ? 'Kamera: 3. LICE ( glatko praćenje aktivnog lika )' : 'Kamera: 1. LICE ( direktan pogled )');\n                        }\n                        break;\n                    case 'Digit1': case 'KeyP': switchCharacter('petar'); break;\n                    case 'Digit2': case 'KeyL': switchCharacter('lazar'); break;\n                    case 'Digit3': switchCharacter('andrej'); break;\n                }\n            });\n\n            document.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => {\n                window._arkKeys[e.code]=false;\n                handleMovementKeys(e.code, false);\n            });\n        }\n\n        function setSubtitle(text) {\n            $('subtitle').textContent = text;\n            if(document.body.classList.contains('cinematic'))arkFilmPresentSubtitle(String(text));\n        }\n\n        function toggleFlashlight() {\n            if (gameOver || missionState !== STATE_SURVIVAL) return;\n            if (battery <= 0) {\n                flashlightOn = false;\n                setSubtitle(\"Baterija je prazna! Pronađi zelene litijumske baterije u snegu.\");\n                return;\n            }\n            flashlightOn = !flashlightOn;\n            playSfx('click');\n        }\n\n        function switchCharacter(targetKey) {\n            if (targetKey === activeChar || gameOver || ![STATE_SURVIVAL,STATE_FLIGHT].includes(missionState)) return;\n            if (gamePhase === 'survival') {\n                // Sačuvaj poziciju dosadašnjeg aktivnog lika na terenu\n                CHAR_PROFILES[activeChar].pos.set(playerPos.x, playerPos.y + 1.8, playerPos.z);\n                companions[activeChar].position.set(playerPos.x, getTerrainHeight(playerPos.x, playerPos.z), playerPos.z);\n                companions[activeChar].visible = true;\n\n                activeChar = targetKey;\n                const nextP = CHAR_PROFILES[activeChar].pos;\n                const gy = getTerrainHeight(nextP.x, nextP.z);\n                playerPos.set(nextP.x, gy, nextP.z);\n                companions[activeChar].position.copy(playerPos);\n                companions[activeChar].visible = thirdPersonMode;\n                velocity.set(0, 0, 0);\n            } else {\n                activeChar = targetKey;\n            }\n\n            ['petar', 'lazar', 'andrej'].forEach((k) => {\n                $('row-' + k).classList.toggle('char-active', k === activeChar);\n            });\n            playSfx('click');\n            setSubtitle(CHAR_PROFILES[activeChar].quote);\n            if(missionState===STATE_SURVIVAL)updateSurvivalHUD();\n        }\n\n        function handleInteraction() {\n            if(missionState===STATE_FLIGHT){ark2TryNavFix();return;}\n            if (missionState !== STATE_SURVIVAL || !focusedItem || gameOver) return;\n            const item = focusedItem;\n            if(['wood','batt','part'].includes(item.type)&&!arkPickupCommit){arkStartPickup(item);return;}\n            charAnimBlend.interact = 0.55;\n\n            if (item.type === 'wood') {\n                item.collected = true;\n                item.mesh.visible = false;\n                woodCount++;\n                playSfx('pickup');\n                setSubtitle(\"Pokupio si suva drva za ogrev. Odnesi ih do vatre u kampu!\");\n            } else if (item.type === 'batt') {\n                item.collected = true;\n                item.mesh.visible = false;\n                battery = Math.min(100, battery + 45);\n                flashlightOn = true;\n                playSfx('pickup');\n                setSubtitle(\"Baterija dopunjena (+45%)! Snop lampe je ponovo stabilan.\");\n            } else if (item.type === 'part') {\n                item.collected = true;\n                item.mesh.visible = false;\n                carriedParts.push(item.kind);\n                playSfx('pickup');\n                setSubtitle(`Pronađeno: ${item.name}! Odnesi do aviona (-7.5, -7.5) da Lazar [2] ugradi deo.`);\n            } else if (item.type === 'fire') {\n                if (woodCount > 0) {\n                    woodCount--;\n                    fireWarmth = Math.min(100, fireWarmth + 28);\n                    petarHealth = Math.min(100, petarHealth + 14);\n                    playSfx('pickup');\n                    setSubtitle(\"Ubacio si drva u vatru. Plamen se razbuktao i greje promrzlog Petra!\");\n                } else {\n                    setSubtitle(\"Nemaš suvih drva u rancu! Potraži ogrev ispod zaleđenih stabala.\");\n                }\n            } else if (item.type === 'plane') {\n                if (planeReady) {\n                    beginTakeoffCutscene();\n                    return;\n                }\n                if (carriedParts.length === 0) {\n                    setSubtitle(`Lazar: 'Na avionu je ugrađeno ${installedCount}/5 delova. Pronađite preostale delove sa svetlosnim stubom u šumi!'`);\n                    return;\n                }\n                // Ako igrač nije na Lazaru, automatski pozovi Lazara da ugradi donesene delove\n                if (activeChar !== 'lazar') {\n                    setSubtitle(\"Pozvao si Lazara [2] do trupa aviona — Lazar preuzima alat i ugrađuje delove!\");\n                }\n                carriedParts.forEach((k) => {\n                    installedMap[k] = (installedMap[k] || 0) + 1;\n                });\n                installedCount += carriedParts.length;\n                carriedParts = [];\n                playSfx('radio');\n                if (installedCount >= 5) {\n                    planeReady = true;\n                }\n                updateAircraftVisuals();\n\n                if (planeReady) {\n                    setSubtitle(\"VUK (RADIO): 'Momci, vidim vaš signal! Popravili ste avion! Pritisnite [E] da ukrcate tim i letite na sever (0, -380)!'\");\n                } else {\n                    setSubtitle(`Lazar je ugradio delove u avion (${installedCount}/5)! Još ${5 - installedCount} do poletanja.`);\n                }\n            } else if (item.type === 'comp_petar') {\n                setSubtitle(\"Petar: 'Samo održavajte vatru... Prebacite na mene tasterom [1] da vidite sve delove aviona na radaru!'\");\n            } else if (item.type === 'comp_lazar') {\n                setSubtitle(\"Lazar: 'Donesite turbinu, hidrauliku, akumulator i 2 kerozina kod trupa aviona da ih povežem!'\");\n            } else if (item.type === 'comp_andrej') {\n                setSubtitle(\"Andrej: 'Prebaci na mene tasterom [3] kad nosiš teške delove iz šume, ja trčim najbrže!'\");\n            }\n\n            updateSurvivalHUD();\n            eliteSave(true);\n        }\n\n        function setCinematic(enabled, chapter) {\n            document.body.classList.toggle('cinematic', enabled);\n            document.body.classList.toggle('playing', !enabled && missionState !== STATE_LOADING && missionState !== STATE_COMPLETE);\n            if(enabled)arkRM=arkReducedMotionNow();\n            $('cin-chapter').textContent=chapter || 'ARHIVA // MISIJA T-76.4';\n            $('cin-fade').style.opacity='0';\n            $('lightning-flash').style.opacity='0';\n            if (enabled) { $('subtitle-box').style.display='flex'; $('hud-right').style.display='none'; }\n            if(enabled){\n                const which=missionState===STATE_INTRO_CUTSCENE?'intro':\n                    missionState===STATE_TAKEOFF_CUTSCENE?'takeoff':\n                    (missionState===STATE_FLIGHT && ark2.landed)?'landing':'';\n                if(which)arkFilmBegin(which);\n            }else{\n                arkFilmEnd();\n                if(renderer)renderer.toneMappingExposure=.94;\n            }\n        }\n\n        function arkReducedMotionNow(){try{return !!(window.matchMedia&&window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches);}catch(_){return false;}}\n        function arkFadeIn(sec){\n            let el=document.getElementById('ark-handoff');\n            if(!el){el=document.createElement('div');el.id='ark-handoff';el.style.cssText='position:fixed;inset:0;background:#000;opacity:0;pointer-events:none;z-index:46';document.body.appendChild(el);}\n            el.style.transition='none';el.style.opacity='1';void el.offsetWidth;\n            el.style.transition='opacity '+(arkRM?sec*.6:sec)+'s ease';el.style.opacity='0';\n        }\n        function arkTitleCard(){\n            if(!document.getElementById('ark-cine-css')){\n                const st=document.createElement('style');st.id='ark-cine-css';\n                st.textContent='@keyframes arkTitle{0%{opacity:0}18%{opacity:1}72%{opacity:1}100%{opacity:0}}#ark-title{position:fixed;left:0;right:0;top:38%;text-align:center;z-index:48;pointer-events:none;color:#e8f6ff;font:700 small{display:block;margin-top:14px;font-size:.34em;letter-spacing:.5em;color:#9ec8d3}@media (pointer:coarse){#cin-skip{min-width:96px;min-height:56px}}';\n                document.head.appendChild(st);\n            }\n            const old=document.getElementById('ark-title');if(old)old.remove();\n            const }\n\n        function beginIntroCutscene(){\n            missionState=STATE_INTRO_CUTSCENE;\n            cinematicTime=0;introAlarmSound=false;introCrashSound=false;\n w.material.opacity=.50+.34*u;\n                w.scale.setScalar(.94+.09*u);\n            }\n            const smooth=(a,b,x)=>{const q=THREE.MathUtils.clamp((x-a)/(b-a),0,1);return q*q*(3-2*q);};\n            const act1=t<7.5,act2=t>=7.5&&t<14,act3=t>=14&&t<22,act4=t>=22;\n            cinemaHangar.visible=act1;cinemaCabin.visible=act2||act3;cinemaAftermath.visible=act4;\n            if(act1){\n if(t>=28)finishIntroCutscene();\n        }\n        function finishIntroCutscene(){\n            if(missionState!==STATE_INTRO_CUTSCENE)return;\n            missionState=STATE_SURVIVAL;gamePhase='survival';\n            cinemaHangar.visible=false;cinemaCabin.visible=false;cinemaAftermath.visible=false;\n            cinemaCabin.rotation.set(0,0,0);cinemaSparks.visible=false;cinemaWarningLight.intensity=0;\n            planeGroup.visible=true;\n            planeGroup.position.copy(PLANE_START_POS);planeGroup.rotation.set(0,.15,0);\n            cabinCrewGroup.visible=false;\n            updateAircraftVisuals();\n            for(const k in companions){\n                const cp = CHAR_PROFILES[k].pos;\n                companions[k].position.set(cp.x, getTerrainHeight(cp.x, cp.z), cp.z);\n                companions[k].visible = (k === activeChar) ?
