playerPos.set(startP.x, getTerrainHeight(startP.x, startP.z), startP.z);\n            yaw = -0.22; pitch = 0.05;\n            activeCharFacing = Math.PI - 0.22;\n            const initFwd = V3(-Math.sin(yaw), 0, -Math.cos(yaw));\n            smoothLookAt.set(playerPos.x + initFwd.x * 6.5, playerPos.y + 1.75, playerPos.z + initFwd.z * 6.5);\n            smoothCamPos.set(playerPos.x - initFwd.x * camDist, playerPos.y + 2.35, playerPos.z - initFwd.z * camDist);\n            camera.position.copy(smoothCamPos);\n            camera.lookAt(smoothLookAt);\n            camera.fov = 60; camera.updateProjectionMatrix();\n            lightningLight.intensity=0;\n            resetPremiumCinematic();\n            if(engineGain)engineGain.gain.value=0;\n            setCinematic(false);\n            $('hud').style.display='block';$('hud-right').style.display='flex';$('controls-bar').style.display='flex';$('reticle').style.display='block';\n            $('subtitle-box').style.display='flex';\n            setSubtitle('NEKAKO SMO PREŽIVELI! Pronađi 5 delova aviona. [1] Petar — radar; [2] Lazar — popravka; [3] Andrej — brzina.');\n            const tc=document.getElementById('ark-title');if(tc)tc.remove();\n            arkFadeIn(1.6);\n        }\n\n        function beginTakeoffCutscene() {\n            if(missionState!==STATE_SURVIVAL||!planeReady)return;\n            missionState=STATE_TAKEOFF_CUTSCENE;cinematicTime=0;\n            takeoffStartingPosition=planeGroup.position.clone();takeoffCap=-1;takeoffGround=takeoffStartingPosition.y-getTerrainHeight(takeoffStartingPosition.x,takeoffStartingPosition.z);takeoffGy=takeoffStartingPosition.y;\n            setCinematic(true,'02 // EVAKUACIJA SA OSTRVA');\n            cabinCrewGroup.visible=true;\n            for(const key in companions)companions[key].visible=false;\n            propellerGroup.visible=true;\n            setSubtitle(\"VUK (RADIO): 'Čujem vaš motor! Držite kurs sever (Z: -380), čekam vas na osvetljenoj pisti!'\");\n            playSfx('radio');\n        }\n\n        function arkSm(a,b,x){const q=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(x-a)/(b-a)));return q*q*(3-2*q);}\n        function updateTakeoffCutscene(dt,elapsed) {\n            cinematicTime+=dt;\n            const T=cinematicTime,S=takeoffStartingPosition,sm=arkSm;\n            const roll=sm(0,6,T),lift=sm(5,9.5,T),u=Math.max(0,T-5);\n            const px=S.x*(1-.38*lift),pz=S.z-(3*T+.75*T*T);\n            takeoffGy+=(getTerrainHeight(px,pz)+takeoffGround-takeoffGy)*Math.min(1,dt*6);\n            planeGroup.position.set(px,takeoffGy+lift*(u*3.2+u*u*.55),pz);\n            planeGroup.rotation.set(.02+.2*lift,.15*(1-roll),Math.sin(T*5)*.015*(1-lift)+Math.sin(T*.8)*.05*lift);\n            propellerGroup.rotation.z-=dt*(14+60*sm(0,3,T));\n            if(engineGain)engineGain.gain.value=.06+.16*sm(0,6,T);\n            if(engineOsc)engineOsc.frequency.value=72+78*sm(0,8,T);\n            const P=planeGroup.position,a=1-sm(3.2,4.4,T),c=sm(7.2,8.4,T),b=1-a-c,w=Math.max(0,T-7.6);\n            const tx=P.x+7.5*a+15*b-12*c,ty=P.y+1.6*a+(3+lift*5)*b+(9+w*3.2)*c,tz=P.z+12.5*a+3*b+(26+w*5)*c;\n            const k=T<.1?1:1-Math.exp(-dt*2.8);\n            camera.position.x+=(tx-camera.position.x)*k;camera.position.y+=(ty-camera.position.y)*k;camera.position.z+=(tz-camera.position.z)*k;\n            if(!arkRM){const sh=.012+.03*roll*(1-lift);camera.position.x+=(Math.random()-.5)*sh;camera.position.y+=(Math.random()-.5)*sh*.7;}\n            camera.lookAt(P.x,P.y+1.3,P.z-2*a);\n            camera.fov=54*a+48*b+64*c;camera.updateProjectionMatrix();\n            const caps=['ANDREJ: \"Gas do daske! Led drži, ovo je naša jedina šansa.\"','LAZAR: \"Brzina raste... još malo... Podiži nos!\"','PETAR: \"Odlepili smo se! Ostrvo ostaje iza nas!\"','VUK (RADIO): \"Čujem vaš motor! Držite kurs sever, čekam vas na osvetljenoj pisti!\"'];\n            const ci=T<3.4?0:T<5.6?1:T<8.4?2:3;\n            if(ci!==takeoffCap){takeoffCap=ci;setSubtitle(caps[ci]);}\n            $('cin-fade').style.opacity=String(Math.max(1-sm(0,.8,T),sm(10.7,11.5,T)));\n            arkFilmTick('takeoff',T,dt);\n            if(T>=11.5)finishTakeoffCutscene();\n        }\n\n        function finishTakeoffCutscene(){\n            if(missionState!==STATE_TAKEOFF_CUTSCENE)return;\n            camera.fov=60;camera.updateProjectionMatrix();\n            launchFlightPhase();\n            setCinematic(false);\n            $('hud-right').style.display='flex';\n            $('controls-bar').style.display='flex';$('subtitle-box').style.display='flex';\n            arkFadeIn(1.3);\n        }\n\n        function beginOutroCutscene() {\n            if(missionState!==STATE_FLIGHT)return;\n            missionState=STATE_OUTRO_CUTSCENE;cinematicTime=0;\n            outroStartingPosition=planePos.clone();outroCap=-1;outroTouch=false;\n            setCinematic(true,'03 // VUKOVA BAZA // SLETANJE');\n            setSubtitle('VUK: \"Vidim vas! Pratite zelena svetla, pista je slobodna!\"');\n            playSfx('radio');\n        }\n\n        function updateOutroCutscene(dt,elapsed) {\n            cinematicTime+=dt;\n            const T=cinematicTime,S=outroStartingPosition,sm=arkSm;\n            const d=sm(0,3.5,T),uu=Math.min(1,T/9),e=1-(1-uu)*(1-uu);\n            const zEnd=Math.min(VUK_BASE_POS.z-54,S.z-18);\n            planeGroup.position.set(S.x*(1-d),Math.max(1.2,S.y*(1-d)+1.3*d),S.z+(zEnd-S.z)*e);\n            planeGroup.rotation.set(-.06*(1-sm(0,2,T))+.07*sm(1.8,3.3,T)*(1-sm(3.4,4.6,T)),flightYaw*(1-sm(0,3,T)),flightRoll*(1-sm(0,2.5,T)));\n            propellerGroup.rotation.z-=dt*(37*(1-sm(3,9,T))+5);\n            if(engineGain)engineGain.gain.value=.08*(1-sm(2,9,T));\n            if(!outroTouch&&T>=3.4){outroTouch=true;cinematicTone(58,.4,'sine',.16);cinematicTone(210,.1,'square',.03);}\n            const P=planeGroup.position,a=1-sm(4.2,5.4,T),c=sm(7.4,8.6,T),b=1-a-c;\n            const tx=(P.x+9)*a+(P.x+13)*b+34*c,ty=(P.y+2.8)*a+(P.y+.9)*b+5.5*c,tz=(P.z+16)*a+(P.z+6)*b+(VUK_BASE_POS.z-10)*c;\n            const k=T<.1?1:1-Math.exp(-dt*3);\n            camera.position.x+=(tx-camera.position.x)*k;camera.position.y+=(ty-camera.position.y)*k;camera.position.z+=(tz-camera.position.z)*k;\n            if(!arkRM&&T>3.4&&T<4.6){const j=Math.exp(-(T-3.4)*5)*.18;camera.position.x+=(Math.random()-.5)*j;camera.position.y+=(Math.random()-.5)*j;}\n            camera.lookAt(P.x,P.y+1.2,P.z-3*a);\n            camera.fov=56*a+46*b+42*c;camera.updateProjectionMatrix();\n            const caps=['VUK (RADIO): \"Vidim vas! Pratite zelena svetla, pista je slobodna!\"','PETAR: \"Smanji gas... nos gore... evo ga, tlo!\"','Točkovi dodiruju led. Avion klizi kroz sneg ka hangaru.','ANDREJ: \"Uspeli smo... Preživeli smo ostrvo i stigli do Vuka!\"','VUK: \"Dobro došli, legende! Kafa je već na stolu.\"'];\n            const ci=T<2.6?0:T<4.2?1:T<7?2:T<9?3:4;\n            if(ci!==outroCap){outroCap=ci;setSubtitle(caps[ci]);}\n            $('cin-fade').style.opacity=String(Math.max(1-sm(0,.7,T),sm(9.4,10.3,T)));\n            if(T>=10.3){\n                camera.fov=60;camera.updateProjectionMatrix();\n                setCinematic(false);\n                triggerEndGame(true,'Petar, Lazar i Andrej su preživeli ostrvo T-76.4, popravili avion i bezbedno sleteli kod Vuka.');\n            }\n        }\n\n        function skipCinematic() {\n            if(missionState===STATE_INTRO_CUTSCENE)finishIntroCutscene();\n            else if(missionState===STATE_TAKEOFF_CUTSCENE)finishTakeoffCutscene();\n            else if(missionState===STATE_FLIGHT && ark2.landed){ark2FinishLanding();}\n            else if(missionState===STATE_OUTRO_CUTSCENE){\n                setCinematic(false);\n                triggerEndGame(true,'Tri drugara su bezbedno sletela kod Vuka.');\n            }\n        }\n\n        function updateLoading(now){\n            if(!loadingStart)loadingStart=now;\n            const p=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(now-loadingStart)/450));\n            const percent=Math.round(p*100);\n            $('load-percent').textContent=String(percent).padStart(3,'0')+'%';\n            $('load-fill').style.width=percent+'%';\n            loggedLines=LOADING_LOGS.length;\n            $('load-log').textContent=LOADING_LOGS.join('\\n');\n            $('start-btn').style.display='block';\n            if(p>=1){$('load-percent').textContent='100%';}\n        }\n\n        // ============================================================================\n        // FAZA 2 // NORVEŠKA — potpuno povezana sa postojećim pogonom.\n        // Ne dira originalni Three.js r128, save niti likove iz Faze 1.\n        // ============================================================================\n        const ARK2_AIRPORT = V3(180, 0, -1500);\n        const ARK2_RUNWAY = {width:44,length:320,nearZ:-1340,farZ:-1660};\n        const ARK2_SAVE = 'arkticki_ruset_norway_flight_v1';\n        const ark2 = {\n            mission:'STORM_PREP',timer:0,storm:0,navReady:false,navFixStarted:false,\n            navFixProgress:0,landed:false,ready:false,roam:false,postTime:0,postStage:-1,\n            dialogue:[],dialogueTime:0,airport:null,aurora:null,lightning:null,\n            clouds:[],mountains:[],policeCars:[],policeRoot:null,vuk:null,ground:null,\n            lastKts:0,lastVertical:0,invalidLandingCooldown:0,checkpointTime:0,\n            sky:null,testMode:false\n        };\n        window._arkKeys = Object.create(null); // Kompatibilno sa postojećim kontrolama.\n        const ark2Clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));\n        function ark2Queue(who,text){ark2.dialogue.push({who,text,shown:false});}\n        function ark2SetMission(stage){\n            ark2.mission=stage; ark2.timer=0;\n            const titles={STORM_PREP:'1. Uleti u snežnu oluju',STORM:'1. Preživi snežnu oluju',\n                NAV_PENDING:'2. Pripremi se za navigacioni kvar',NAV_FAIL:'2. Lazar [2] mora da popravi navigaciju [E]',\n                APPROACH:'3. Pronađi aerodrom i bezbedno sleti',LANDED:'4. Susret sa Vukom'};\n            $('mission-title').textContent='::: FAZA 2 — PUT DO NORVEŠKE :::';\n            $('obj-1').textContent='1. Prolaz kroz oluju';\n            $('obj-2').textContent='2. Popravi navigaciju (Lazar [2] + E)';\n            $('obj-3').textContent='3. Pronađi osvetljenu pistu';\n            $('obj-4').textContent='4. Sleti: ≤ 75 KTS, visina ≤ 3 m';\n            const done=stage==='STORM_PREP'?0:stage==='STORM'?0:stage==='NAV_PENDING'||stage==='NAV_FAIL'?1:stage==='APPROACH'?2:4;\n            ['obj-1','obj-2','obj-3','obj-4'].forEach((id,i)=>$(id).className=i<done?'obj-done':i===done?'obj-active':'');\n            if(stage==='NAV_FAIL'){\n                $('avionics-overlay').style.display='none';\n                $('flight-hud-left').style.opacity='.55';\n            } else {\n                $('avionics-overlay').style.display='block';\n                $('flight-hud-left').style.opacity='1';\n            }\n            if(stage==='APPROACH')ark2Queue('VUK (RADIO)','Vidim vas na radaru! Poravnajte se sa osvetljenom pistom!');\n            ark2SaveCheckpoint();\n        }\n        function ark2SaveCheckpoint(){\n            if(!gameStarted||gameOver||missionState!==STATE_FLIGHT||ark2.landed)return;\n            try{localStorage.setItem(ARK2_SAVE,JSON.stringify({v:1,ts:Date.now(),pos:planePos.toArray(),\n                yaw:flightYaw,pitch:flightPitch,roll:flightRoll,speed:airspeed,throttle, hull:planeHull,\n                mission:ark2.mission,navReady:ark2.navReady,navProgress:ark2.navFixProgress,\n                flightTimeSec,collisionHits,activeChar}));}catch(_){ }\n        }\n        function ark2ReadCheckpoint(){\n            try{const x=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(ARK2_SAVE)||'null');\n                return x&&x.v===1&&Array.isArray(x.pos)&&x.pos.length===3&&x.pos.every(Number.isFinite)&&\n                       ['STORM_PREP','STORM','NAV_PENDING','NAV_FAIL','APPROACH'].includes(x.mission)?x:null;\n            }catch(_){return null;}\n        }\n        function ark2RestoreCheckpoint(x){\n            if(!x)return false;\n            launchFlightPhase();\n            planePos.fromArray(x.pos); flightYaw=Number(x.yaw)||0;flightPitch=Number(x.pitch)||0;\n            flightRoll=Number(x.roll)||0;airspeed=ark2Clamp(Number(x.speed)||45,15,94);\n            throttle=throttleLvl=ark2Clamp(Number(x.throttle)||.6,.05,1);\n            planeHull=ark2Clamp(Number(x.hull)||100,1,100);\n            flightTimeSec=Math.max(0,Number(x.flightTimeSec)||0);\n            collisionHits=Math.max(0,Number(x.collisionHits)||0);\n            activeChar=CHAR_PROFILES[x.activeChar]?x.activeChar:'andrej';\n            ark2.navReady=Boolean(x.navReady);ark2.navFixProgress=Math.max(0,Number(x.navProgress)||0);\n            ark2SetMission(x.mission); ark2.dialogue.length=0;\n            planeGroup.position.copy(planePos); planeGroup.rotation.set(flightPitch,flightYaw,flightRoll,'YXZ');\n            setSubtitle('Sačuvani let je učitan — nastavi prema Norveškoj.');\n            return true;\n        }\n        function ark2AddMesh(parent,geo,mat,x,y,z){const m=new THREE.Mesh(geo,mat);m.position.set(x,y,z);parent.add(m);return m;}\n        function ark2BuildWorld(){\n            const group=new THREE.Group();group.position.copy(ARK2_AIRPORT);\n            ark2.airport=group;\n            const snowMat=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0xc5deeb,roughness:.96});\n            const asphalt=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x252f3a,roughness:.91});\n            const white=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xe5f5ff});\n            const stripe=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xffe5a0});\n            const ground=ark2AddMesh(group,new THREE.PlaneGeometry(880,850),snowMat,0,-.09,0);\n            ground.rotation.x=-PI/2;ground.receiveShadow=true;ark2.ground=ground;\n            const runway=ark2AddMesh(group,new THREE.BoxGeometry(ARK2_RUNWAY.width,.20,ARK2_RUNWAY.length),asphalt,0,.08,0);\n            runway.receiveShadow=true;\n            // Bočne ivice, centralne crte i pravilan pristup sa juga.\n            for(const side of [-1,1]){\n                ark2AddMesh(group,new THREE.BoxGeometry(.35,.22,ARK2_RUNWAY.length),white,\n                    side*ARK2_RUNWAY.width/2,.19,0);\n            }\n            for(let z=-145;z<=145;z+=25)ark2AddMesh(group,new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.2,.23,11),white,0,.21,z);\n            for(let z=-152;z<=152;z+=10){\n                for(const side of [-1,1]){\n                    const x=side*(ARK2_RUNWAY.width/2+2);\n                    const bulb=ark2AddMesh(group,new THREE.SphereGeometry(.43,7,6),\n                        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:side===1?0x23df75:0xf25454}),x,.45,z);\n                    bulb.userData.runwayBulb=true;\n                }\n            }\n            for(let i=0;i<7;i++){\n                const zz=172+i*18;\n                for(const side of [-1,1]){\n                    ark2AddMesh(group,new THREE.BoxGeometry(.28,.2,1.5),\n                        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0x6ef7ff}),side*(12+i*3),.2,zz);\n                }\n            }\n            const terminal=ark2AddMesh(group,new THREE.BoxGeometry(72,11,33),\n                new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x344a61,roughness:.8}),-78,5.5,-12);\n            terminal.castShadow=true;\n            for(let i=0;i<8;i++)for(let j=0;j<2;j++){\n                const windowMesh=ark2AddMesh(group,new THREE.PlaneGeometry(5,2.1),\n                    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xfde5ad}),-109+i*8,4+j*3,-28.55);\n            }\n            ark2AddMesh(group,new THREE.CylinderGeometry(3.2,3.5,30,10),\n                new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x65768d}),75,15,-24);\n            ark2AddMesh(group,new THREE.BoxGeometry(11,5,11),\n                new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0x8be5ff}),75,32.5,-24);\n            const pole=ark2AddMesh(group,new THREE.SphereGeometry(.65,10,8),\n                new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0x24ff9b}),75,36,-24);\n            ark2.towerBeacon=pole;\n            const light=new THREE.PointLight(0xfce5b0,1.4,135);light.position.set(-65,19,-12);group.add(light);\n            scene.add(group);group.visible=false;\n            // Prateći 3D teren duž maršrute: otvoren vazdušni koridor, bez planina kod piste.\n            const rock=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x697c8a,roughness:1});\n            const cap=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0xe5f0f6,roughness:.87});\n            for(let i=0;i<19;i++){\n                const z=-210-i*55;\n                const corridorX=ARK2_AIRPORT.x*ark2Clamp((-z-110)/1300,0,1);\n                for(const sign of [-1,1]){\n                    const x=corridorX+sign*(150+Math.random()*90);\n                    const h=58+Math.random()*84,r=31+Math.random()*29;\n                    const mountain=ark2AddMesh(scene,new THREE.ConeGeometry(r,h,7),rock,x,h*.48,z);\n                    const snowcap=ark2AddMesh(scene,new THREE.ConeGeometry(r*.42,h*.30,7),cap,x,h*.85,z);\n                    ark2.mountains.push({x,z,r:r*.9,height:h});\n                    snowcap.castShadow=false;mountain.castShadow=false;\n                }\n            }\n            const cloudMat=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xe2f6ff,transparent:true,opacity:.21,\n                depthWrite:false,side:THREE.DoubleSide, fog:true});\n            for(let i=0;i<24;i++){\n                const z=-200-Math.random()*1300;\n                const cl=ark2AddMesh(scene,new THREE.PlaneGeometry(88+Math.random()*95,27+Math.random()*36),\n                    cloudMat.clone(),(Math.random()-.5)*470,54+Math.random()*76,z);\n                cl.rotation.y=(Math.random()-.5)*.7;ark2.clouds.push(cl);\n            }\n            const aurora=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(730,190,30,5),new THREE.ShaderMaterial({\n                transparent:true,depthWrite:false,side:THREE.DoubleSide,fog:false,\n                uniforms:{uTime:sharedUniforms.time},\n                vertexShader:'varying vec2 vUv; void main(){vUv=uv;gl_Position=projectionMatrix*modelViewMatrix*vec4(position,1.0);}',\n                fragmentShader:'varying vec2 vUv;uniform float uTime;void main(){float s=sin(vUv.x*10.+uTime*.32)*.5+.5;float y=smoothstep(0.,.25,vUv.y)*(1.-smoothstep(.7,1.,vUv.y));vec3 c=mix(vec3(.08,.9,.55),vec3(.52,.42,1.),s);gl_FragColor=vec4(c,(.2+.8*s)*y*.45);}'\n            }));\n            aurora.position.set(35,150,-950);aurora.rotation.x=-PI/2.65;\n            aurora.visible=false;scene.add(aurora);ark2.aurora=aurora;\n            const stormLight=new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xd2eaff,0);stormLight.position.set(60,150,-500);\n            scene.add(stormLight);ark2.lightning=stormLight;\n            // Proceduralna nebeska kupola iz Faze 1 mora pratiti avion kada napusti ostrvo.\n            scene.traverse(obj=>{if(obj.geometry?.type==='SphereGeometry'&&obj.geometry.parameters?.radius===1100&&obj.material?.isShaderMaterial)ark2.sky=obj;});\n        }\n        function ark2Start(){\n            ark2.timer=0;ark2.storm=0;ark2.navReady=false;ark2.navFixStarted=false;\n            ark2.navFixProgress=0;ark2.landed=false;ark2.ready=false;ark2.roam=false;\n            ark2.postTime=0;ark2.postStage=-1;ark2.dialogue.length=0;\n            ark2.dialogueTime=0;ark2.invalidLandingCooldown=0;ark2.checkpointTime=0;\n            ark2.airport.visible=true; ark2.aurora.visible=true;\n            planePos.set(0,46,-110); flightYaw=0;flightPitch=0.06;flightRoll=0;\n            flightPitchRate=0;flightYawRate=0;\n            airspeed=39; throttle=throttleLvl=.64;planeHull=100;hitCooldown=2;\n            planeGroup.visible=true;planeGroup.position.copy(planePos);\n            planeGroup.rotation.set(flightPitch,flightYaw,flightRoll,'YXZ');\n            cabinCrewGroup.visible=true;cockpitFrameGroup.visible=false;\n            ark2SetMission('STORM_PREP');\n            ark2Queue('ANDREJ','Uzleteli smo! Sada samo da pronađemo Norvešku.');\n            ark2Queue('PETAR','Na karti piše hiljadu i po metara do piste.');\n            ark2Queue('LAZAR','Turbina radi! Ali radio još uvek krči...');\n            setSubtitle('FAZA 2: Upravljaj avionom do Vukovog aerodroma.');\n        }\n        function ark2TryNavFix(){\n            if(missionState!==STATE_FLIGHT||ark2.mission!=='NAV_FAIL')return false;\n            if(!ark2.navReady){setSubtitle('Sačekaj da Lazar pripremi navigacioni panel.');return true;}\n            if(activeChar!=='lazar'){\n                setSubtitle('Navigaciju može da popravi LAZAR — pritisni [2], pa [E].');\n                return true;\n            }\n            if(!ark2.navFixStarted){\n                ark2.navFixStarted=true;ark2.navFixProgress=0;playSfx('click');\n                setSubtitle('LAZAR: „Restartujem navigaciju! Drži avion stabilno!“');\n            }\n            return true;\n        }\n        function ark2QueueTick(dt){\n            if(!ark2.dialogue.length)return;\n            ark2.dialogueTime+=dt;\n            const cur=ark2.dialogue[0];\n            if(!cur.shown&&ark2.dialogueTime>=.65){\n                cur.shown=true;ark2.dialogueTime=0;setSubtitle(cur.who+': „'+cur.text+'“');\n                if(cur.who.includes('RADIO'))playSfx('radio');\n            }else if(cur.shown&&ark2.dialogueTime>=3.4){ark2.dialogue.shift();ark2.dialogueTime=0;}\n        }\n        function ark2MissionTick(dt,elapsed){\n            ark2.timer+=dt;\n            if(ark2.mission==='STORM_PREP'&&planePos.z< -310){\n                ark2SetMission('STORM');ark2Queue('ANDREJ','Ulazimo u oluju! Pripremite se!');\n                ark2Queue('PETAR','Ne vidim horizont! Samo sneg i munje!');\n            }\n            if(ark2.mission==='STORM'){\n                const t=ark2Clamp((-planePos.z-310)/420,0,1);\n                ark2.storm=Math.sin(t*PI);\n                planePos.x+=Math.sin(elapsed*7)*ark2.storm*dt*1.8;\n                planePos.y+=Math.sin(elapsed*5.3)*ark2.storm*dt*1.2;\n                flightRoll+=Math.sin(elapsed*6)*ark2.storm*dt*.10;\n                if(ark2.lightning&&Math.random()<dt*.20*ark2.storm){\n                    ark2.lightning.intensity=5.0;\n                    $('lightning-flash').style.opacity='.38';\n                }\n                if(planePos.z < -740){\n                    ark2SetMission('NAV_PENDING');ark2Queue('PETAR','Oluja je iza nas! Pogledajte instrumente...');\n                }\n            }\n            if(ark2.mission==='NAV_PENDING'&&planePos.z < -820){\n                ark2.navReady=false;ark2.navFixStarted=false;ark2SetMission('NAV_FAIL');\n                ark2Queue('PETAR','Andrej! Navigacija je otkazala!');\n                ark2Queue('ANDREJ','Lazar, mogu da održavam pravac. Popravi je!');\n                ark2Queue('LAZAR','Pritisni [2], zatim [E] da otvorim panel.');\n                playSfx('crash');\n            }\n            if(ark2.mission==='NAV_FAIL'){\n                if(ark2.timer>2.2)ark2.navReady=true;\n                if(ark2.navFixStarted){\n                    ark2.navFixProgress=ark2Clamp(ark2.navFixProgress+dt/3.8,0,1);\n                    $('subtitle').textContent='LAZAR // POPRAVKA NAVIGACIJE: '+Math.round(ark2.navFixProgress*100)+'%';\n                    if(ark2.navFixProgress>=1){\n                        ark2SetMission('APPROACH');\n                        ark2Queue('LAZAR','Sistem radi! Vidim aerodromska svetla!');\n                        ark2Queue('VUK (RADIO)','Dobro! Poravnajte avion sa pistom, smanjite gas i sletite.');\n                        playSfx('radio');\n                    }\n                } else if(ark2.navReady&&Math.round(ark2.timer)%7===0&&ark2.timer<25){\n                    $('runway-guide-msg').textContent='KVAR NAVIGACIJE // LAZAR [2], ZATIM [E]';\n                }\n            }\n            if(ark2.mission==='APPROACH' && planePos.z > -1250 && ark2.timer<4){\n                $('runway-guide-msg').textContent='VIDIŠ PISTU U DALJINI — SPREMI PRILAZ';\n            }\n            if(ark2.lightning)ark2.lightning.intensity*=Math.max(0,1-dt*6);\n            const fade=$('lightning-flash');fade.style.opacity=String(Math.max(0,(Number(fade.style.opacity)||0)-dt*2.6));\n        }\n        function ark2BeginLanding(){\n            if(ark2.landed)return;\n            ark2.landed=true;ark2.postTime=0;ark2.postStage=-1;\n            ark2SetMission('LANDED');\n            // Zadrži poziciju na pisti, ali više ne izračunavaj fiziku leta.\n            planePos.y=1.7;airspeed=0;throttle=throttleLvl=0;\n            planeGroup.position.copy(planePos);planeGroup.rotation.set(0,flightYaw,0);\n            for(const c of Object.values(companions))c.visible=false;\n            setCinematic(true,'NORVEŠKA // AERODROM // SLETANJE');\n            $('flight-hud-left').style.display='none';$('avionics-overlay').style.display='none';\n            ark2SpawnVukPolice();\n            arkFilmPrepareLanding();\n            try{localStorage.removeItem(ARK2_SAVE);}catch(_){ }\n            ark2NextShot(0);\n        }\n        function ark2SpawnVukPolice(){\n            if(!ark2.vuk){\n                const v=createSurvivorModel(0x2554a4,'V');\n                v.position.set(ARK2_AIRPORT.x+8,.08,ARK2_AIRPORT.z+12);\n                v.rotation.y=PI;scene.add(v);ark2.vuk=v;\n            }\n            ark2.vuk.visible=true;\n            if(!ark2.policeRoot){\n                ark2.policeRoot=new THREE.Group();scene.add(ark2.policeRoot);\n                const dark=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x203c59,metalness:.4,roughness:.5});\n                const glass=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x89bacd,metalness:.3,roughness:.32});\n                for(let i=0;i<2;i++){\n                    const car=new THREE.Group();\n                    ark2AddMesh(car,new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.4,1.2,4.7),dark,0,1.0,0);\n                    ark2AddMesh(car,new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.0,.85,2.2),glass,0,1.9,-.35);\n                    const red=ark2AddMesh(car,new THREE.BoxGeometry(.40,.28,.38),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xff2121}),-.35,2.45,-.25);\n                    const blue=ark2AddMesh(car,new THREE.BoxGeometry(.40,.28,.38),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0x2567ff}),.35,2.45,-.25);\n                    for(const x of [-.95,.95])for(const z of [-1.45,1.45]){\n                        const wheel=ark2AddMesh(car,new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.48,.48,.22,12),\n                            new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x11151a}),x,.5,z);\n                        wheel.rotation.z=PI/2;\n                    }\n                    car.userData.red=red;car.userData.blue=blue;\n                    car.position.set(ARK2_AIRPORT.x-34-i*7,0,ARK2_AIRPORT.z+65+i*12);\n                    ark2.policeRoot.add(car);ark2.policeCars.push(car);\n                }\n            }\n            ark2.policeRoot.visible=false;\n        }\n        function ark2NextShot(stage){\n            if(ark2.postStage===stage)return;\n            ark2.postStage=stage;\n            const caps=[\n                'PETAR: „Uspeli smo! Vidim Vuka! Ne verujem da smo živi!“',\n                'VUK: „Andrej, Lazare, Petre! Dobro došli, ljudi! Kako ste se izvukli?“',\n                'LAZAR: „Nešto nas je pratilo. U olupini smo našli uređaj T-76.4.“',\n                'VUK: „Šta?! Policija?! Kako su nas tako brzo pronašli?!“',\n                'ANDREJ: „Smiri se, Vuče. Moramo da saznamo ko šalje signal.“'\n            ];\n            if(stage===3)ark2.policeRoot.visible=true;\n            setSubtitle(caps[stage]||caps[4]);\n        }\n        function ark2LandingCutscene(dt,elapsed){\n            if(!ark2.landed)return;\n            ark2.postTime+=dt;\n            const t=ark2.postTime;\n            const stage=t<4.2?0:t<9?1:t<12?2:t<18.2?3:4;\n            ark2NextShot(stage);\n            arkFilmAnimateLanding(t,dt,elapsed);\n            if(t>=23.3)ark2FinishLanding();\n        }\n        function ark2FinishLanding(){\n            if(ark2.ready)return;\n            ark2.ready=true;ark2.landed=true;missionState=STATE_PHASE3_READY;\n            setCinematic(false);\n            $('cin-fade').style.opacity='0';\n            $('flight-hud-left').style.display='none';$('avionics-overlay').style.display='none';\n            $('hud-right').style.display='none';$('controls-bar').style.display='none';\n            $('reticle').style.display='none';\n            if(document.pointerLockElement)try{document.exitPointerLock();}catch(_){ }\n            const old=document.getElementById('ark-phase3-ready');if(old)old.remove();\n            const layer=document.createElement('div');layer.id='ark-phase3-ready';\n            layer.style.cssText='position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:160;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:20px;background:rgba(2,9,20,.94);backdrop-filter:blur(14px);color:#e7f6ff;font:14px/1.7 system-ui,sans-serif;text-align:center';\n            layer.innerHTML='<div style=\"max-width:620px;padding:32px;border:1px solid #62d8ff75;border-radius:15px;background:#071729\">'+\n                '<p style=\"color:#69ddff;letter-spacing:3px;font:700 11px monospace\">POGLAVLJE 02 ZAVRŠENO<\/p>'+\n                '<h1 style=\"font-size:clamp(25px,5vw,43px);margin:12px 0\">STIGLI STE U NORVEŠKU<\/h1>'+\n                '<p>Avion je sleteo. Sledeće poglavlje stiže u FAZI 3.');\n            };\n            $('ark2-replay').onclick=()=>{\n                layer.remove();ark2.ready=false;ark2.roam=false;ark2.landed=true;\n      function ark2ReadyRoam(dt,elapsed){\n            if(!ark2.roam)return;\n            const dirX=Number(moveR)-Number(moveL),dirZ=Number(moveB)-Number(moveF);\n            const length=Math.hypot(dirX,dirZ)||1;\n            const speed=isRunning?8:4.3;\n            playerPos.x=ark2Clamp(playerPos.x+dirX/length*speed*dt,ARK2_AIRPORT.x-32,ARK2_AIRPORT.x+30);\n            playerPos.z=ark2Clamp(playerPos.z+dirZ/length*speed*dt,ARK2_AIRPORT.z-110,ARK2_AIRPORT.z+80);\n            playerPos.y=.07;\n            const c=companions.andrej;\n            if(c){c.position.copy(playerPos);c.visible=true;\n                if(dirX||dirZ)c.rotation.y=Math.atan2(dirX,-dirZ);\n                animateCharacter(c,elapsed,(dirX||dirZ)?(isRunning?'run':'walk'):'idle');}\n            const off=V3(Math.sin(yaw)*6,3.5-Math.sin(pitch)*2,Math.cos(yaw)*6);\n            camera.position.lerp(playerPos.clone().add(off),Math.min(1,dt*5));\n            camera.lookAt(playerPos.x,playerPos.y+1.7,playerPos.z);\n            if(ark2.vuk)animateCharacter(ark2.vuk,elapsed,'wave');\n        }\n        function ark2FlightUpdate(dt,elapsed){\n            if(ark2.landed){ark2LandingCutscene(dt,elapsed);return;}\n            ark2.invalidLandingCooldown=Math.max(0,ark2.invalidLandingCooldown-dt);\n            ark2.checkpointTime+=dt;\n            if(ark2.checkpointTime>20){ark2.checkpointTime=0;ark2SaveCheckpoint();}\n            hitCooldown=Math.max(0,hitCooldown-dt);flightTimeSec+=dt;\n            const prevY=planePos.y;\n            const pitchInput=Number(moveF)-Number(moveB);\n            const yawInput=Number(moveL)-Number(moveR);\n            throttleLvl=ark2Clamp(throttleLvl+((isRunning?1:0)-(isBraking?1.5:0))*.43*dt,.05,1);\n            throttle=throttleLvl;\n            flightPitchRate+=(pitchInput*.78-flightPitchRate)*Math.min(1,dt*3);\n            flightPitch+=flightPitchRate*dt;\n            if(!pitchInput)flightPitch*=Math.max(0,1-dt*(eliteFlightAssist?1.15:.5));\n            flightPitch=ark2Clamp(flightPitch,-.56,.56);\n            flightYawRate+=(yawInput*.43-flightYawRate)*Math.min(1,dt*3);\n            flightYaw+=flightYawRate*dt;\n            const turb=Math.sin(elapsed*1.9)*.009+Math.sin(elapsed*3.5)*.012;\n            flightRoll+=(flightYawRate*1.05+ark2.storm*.09*Math.sin(elapsed*9)+turb-flightRoll)*Math.min(1,dt*3);\n            const targetSpeed=15+throttle*65;\n            airspeed+=(targetSpeed-airspeed)*Math.min(1,dt*.95);\n            airspeed-=Math.sin(flightPitch)*12*dt;\n            airspeed=ark2Clamp(airspeed,16,94);\n            const c=Math.cos(flightPitch);\n            const dir=V3(-Math.sin(flightYaw)*c,Math.sin(flightPitch),-Math.cos(flightYaw)*c);\n            planePos.addScaledVector(dir,airspeed*dt);\n            planePos.y+=(Math.sin(elapsed*3.5)*ark2.storm*.7)*dt;\n            planePos.y=Math.min(180,planePos.y);\n            // Otvoren koridor, samo stvarni bliski sudari sa planinama.\n            for(const m of ark2.mountains){\n                const dist=Math.hypot(planePos.x-m.x,planePos.z-m.z);\n                if(dist<m.r*.6 && planePos.y<m.height*.72 && hitCooldown<=0){\n                    damageAircraft(15,'Upozorenje: udar u planinski greben!');\n                    hitCooldown=2.5;planePos.y+=12;\n                }\n            }\n            ark2MissionTick(dt,elapsed);\n            const inRunway=Math.abs(planePos.x-ARK2_AIRPORT.x)<=ARK2_RUNWAY.width/2-2.5 &&\n                 planePos.z<=ARK2_RUNWAY.nearZ-4 && planePos.z>=ARK2_RUNWAY.farZ+4;\n            const onApproach=ark2.mission==='APPROACH';\n            const verticalSpeed=(planePos.y-prevY)/Math.max(dt,.001);\n            ark2.lastVertical=verticalSpeed;\n            const currentKts=airspeed*1.943844492;ark2.lastKts=currentKts;\n            const heading=Math.abs(Math.atan2(Math.sin(flightYaw),Math.cos(flightYaw)));\n            if(inRunway&&planePos.y<=2.9&&onApproach){\n                const stable=currentKts>=34&&currentKts<=75&&verticalSpeed>=-7.5&&\n                    Math.abs(flightRoll)<.30&&heading<.35;\n                if(stable){ark2BeginLanding();return;}\n                if(ark2.invalidLandingCooldown<=0){\n                    ark2.invalidLandingCooldown=2.5;planePos.y=8.5;\n                    damageAircraft(8,'NESTABILAN PRILAZ! Drži pistu, 35–75 KTS i blag silazak.');\n                }\n            }else if(planePos.y<1.6){\n                planePos.y=10.5;flightPitch=.18;\n                if(ark2.invalidLandingCooldown<=0){\n                    ark2.invalidLandingCooldown=2.5;\n                    damageAircraft(16,'SLETANJE VAN PISTE! Vrati se na osvetljenu stazu.');\n                }\n            }\n            if(planeHull<=0){triggerEndGame(false,'Avion je pretrpeo kritična oštećenja.');return;}\n            planeGroup.position.copy(planePos);\n            planeGroup.rotation.set(flightPitch,flightYaw,flightRoll,'YXZ');\n            cockpitFrameGroup.visible=cockpitView;\n            if(cockpitView){camera.position.copy(V3(0,2.25,-2.15).applyQuaternion(planeGroup.quaternion).add(planePos));camera.quaternion.copy(planeGroup.quaternion);}\n            else{camera.position.lerp(V3(0,4.5,18).applyQuaternion(planeGroup.quaternion).add(planePos),Math.min(1,dt*4.5));\n                 camera.lookAt(V3(0,1.2,-20).applyQuaternion(planeGroup.quaternion).add(planePos));}\n            const nose=dir.clone().multiplyScalar(6).add(planePos);\n            sharedUniforms.lightPos.value.copy(nose);sharedUniforms.lightDir.value.copy(dir);\n            flashlight.position.copy(nose);flashlight.target.position.copy(nose).addScaledVector(dir,45);\n            if(engineOsc&&engineGain&&audioCtx){\n                engineOsc.frequency.setTargetAtTime(55+airspeed*.9+throttle*16,audioCtx.currentTime,.2);\n                engineGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(.05+throttle*.08,audioCtx.currentTime,.2);\n            }\n            $('fl-speed').textContent=Math.round(currentKts);\n            $('fl-alt').textContent=Math.max(0,Math.round(planePos.y));\n            $('fl-hull').textContent=Math.max(0,Math.round(planeHull));\n            $('fl-hull-bar').style.width=Math.max(0,Math.round(planeHull))+'%';\n            $('fl-thr').textContent=Math.round(throttle*100);\n            $('fl-thr-bar').style.width=Math.round(throttle*100)+'%';\n            const dist=Math.hypot(planePos.x-ARK2_AIRPORT.x,planePos.z-ARK2_AIRPORT.z);\n            $('fl-dist').textContent=Math.round(dist);\n            $('horizon-line').style.transform=`translateY(${flightPitch*55}px) rotate(${-flightRoll*45}deg)`;\n            const targetX=ARK2_AIRPORT.x,targetZ=ARK2_AIRPORT.z+120;\n            const aim=Math.atan2(-(targetX-planePos.x),-(targetZ-planePos.z));\n            const diff=Math.atan2(Math.sin(aim-flightYaw),Math.cos(aim-flightYaw));\n            const angle=Math.round(Math.abs(diff)*180/PI);\n            $('vuk-bearing-indicator').textContent=ark2.mission==='NAV_FAIL'?'NAVIGACIJA U KVARU — LAZAR [2] + E':\n                `AERODROM ${Math.round(dist)}m // ${angle<5?'DRŽI KURS':diff>0?'LEVO [A] '+angle+'°':'DESNO [D] '+angle+'°'}`;\n            $('runway-guide-msg').textContent=ark2.mission==='NAV_FAIL'?'KVAR NAVIGACIJE — [2] LAZAR, [E] POPRAVKA':\n               `PISTA: X ${Math.round(ARK2_AIRPORT.x-planePos.x)}m / VISINA ${Math.round(planePos.y)}m / ${Math.round(currentKts)} KTS · ZA SLETANJE 35–75 KTS`;\n            ark2QueueTick(dt);\n            if(ark2.towerBeacon)ark2.towerBeacon.material.color.setHex(Math.sin(elapsed*3)>0?0x25ff8c:0x07512d);\n            if(ark2.sky)ark2.sky.position.copy(camera.position);\n            drawRadar();\n        }\n        if (location.search.includes('ark_test=1')) {\n            window.arkPhase2Test={\n                state:()=>({missionState,phase:ark2.mission,activeChar,navReady:ark2.navReady,\n                    navFixStarted:ark2.navFixStarted,navProgress:ark2.navFixProgress,landed:ark2.landed,\n                    ready:ark2.ready,roam:ark2.roam,pos:planePos.toArray(),playerPos:playerPos.toArray(),speed:airspeed}),\n                enter:()=>{if(!gameStarted)window.arkStart3DNow(true);launchFlightPhase();},\n                takeoff:()=>{if(!gameStarted)window.arkStart3DNow(true);installedCount=5;planeReady=true;updateAircraftVisuals();beginTakeoffCutscene();},\n setPhysics:(o)=>{if(Number.isFinite(o.throttle))throttle=throttleLvl=o.throttle;\n                    if(Number.isFinite(o.pitch))flightPitch=o.pitch;if(Number.isFinite(o.yaw))flightYaw=o.yaw;},\n                navFix:()=>ark2TryNavFix(),\n                step:(sec=1)=>{for(let i=0;i<Math.ceil(sec*60);i++)ark2FlightUpdate(1/60,i/60);},\n                landing:()=>ark2BeginLanding(),\n                skipLanding:()=>ark2FinishLanding()\n            };\n        }\n\n\n        function launchFlightPhase() {\n            missionState=STATE_FLIGHT;\n            gamePhase='flight';\n            $('controls-bar').style.display='flex';\n            $('frost-vignette').style.boxShadow='none';\n            for(const k in companions)companions[k].visible=false;\n LICE)':'TREĆE LICE';};\n            ark2Start();\n        }\n\n        \n\n        // ============================================================================\n        // 5. HUD, TAKTIČKI RADAR I ZAVRŠNI EKRAN\n        // ============================================================================\n        function updateSurvivalHUD() {\n            const b = Math.round(battery);\n            const isLow = b < 35;\n            $('batt-val').textContent = b + '%';\n            $('batt-bar').style.width = b + '%';\n            $('batt-val').style.color = $('batt-bar').style.background = isLow ? '#ef4444' : '#22c55e';\n            $('flicker-warn').style.display = isLow ? 'inline' : 'none';\n\n            const f = Math.round(fireWarmth);\n            $('fire-val').textContent = f + '%';\n            $('fire-bar').style.width = f + '%';\n\n            const w = Math.round(playerWarmth);\n            $('warmth-val').textContent = w + '%';\n            $('warmth-bar').style.width = w + '%';\n\n            const frostIntensity = Math.max(0, (65 - playerWarmth) / 65) * 0.78;\n            $('frost-vignette').style.boxShadow = `inset 0 0 115px rgba(56, 189, 248, ${frostIntensity})`;\n\n            const pEl = $('status-petar');\n            if (petarHealth > 70) {\n                pEl.textContent = 'UGREJAN (Stabilno)';\n                pEl.style.color = '#4ade80';\n            } else if (petarHealth > 35) {\n                pEl.textContent = 'OSLABLJEN (Promrzline)';\n                pEl.style.color = '#fca5a5';\n            } else {\n                pEl.textContent = 'KRITIČNO (Hipotermija!)';\n                pEl.style.color = '#ef4444';\n            }\n\n            $('inv-wood').textContent = woodCount;\n            $('inv-bag').textContent = carriedParts.length;\n            $('inv-plane').textContent = `${installedCount}/5`;\n\n            const totalFound = installedCount + carriedParts.length;\n            $('obj-2').textContent = `2. Pronađi delove aviona u šumi (${totalFound}/5)`;\n            $('obj-2').className = totalFound >= 5 ? 'obj-done' : 'obj-active';\n            $('obj-3').className = planeReady ? 'obj-done' : (carriedParts.length > 0 ? 'obj-active' : '');\n            $('obj-4').className = planeReady ? 'obj-active' : '';\n        }\n\n        function drawRadar() {\n            const canvas = $('radar-canvas');\n            const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n            const cx = 68, cy = 68;\n            const isFlight = (gamePhase === 'flight');\n            const heading = isFlight ? flightYaw : yaw;\n            const origin = isFlight ? planePos : playerPos;\n            const range = isFlight ? (activeChar === 'petar' ? 340 : 210) : CHAR_PROFILES[activeChar].radarRange;\n            const scale = 58 / range;\n            const cosH = Math.cos(heading);\n            const sinH = Math.sin(heading);\n\n            ctx.clearRect(0, 0, 136, 136);\n            ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.28)';\n            ctx.lineWidth = 1;\n            ctx.beginPath();\n            ctx.arc(cx, cy, 28, 0, PI * 2);\n            ctx.moveTo(cx + 56, cy);\n            ctx.arc(cx, cy, 56, 0, PI * 2);\n            ctx.moveTo(8, cy); ctx.lineTo(128, cy);\n            ctx.moveTo(cx, 8); ctx.lineTo(cx, 128);\n            ctx.stroke();\n\n            const plotBlip = (wx, wz, color, rad) => {\n                const dx = wx - origin.x;\n                const dz = wz - origin.z;\n                if (Math.hypot(dx, dz) > range) return;\n                const rx = dx * cosH - dz * sinH;\n                const rz = dx * sinH + dz * cosH;\n                ctx.fillStyle = color;\n                ctx.beginPath();\n                ctx.arc(cx + rx * scale, cy + rz * scale, rad, 0, PI * 2);\n                ctx.fill();\n            };\n\n            if (isFlight) {\n                // Iscrtaj ledene bregove\n                ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.7)';\n                icebergs.forEach((b) => {\n                    const dx = b.x - origin.x;\n                    const dz = b.z - origin.z;\n                    if (Math.hypot(dx, dz) > range + b.r) return;\n                    const rx = dx * cosH - dz * sinH;\n                    const rz = dx * sinH + dz * cosH;\n                    ctx.beginPath();\n                    ctx.arc(cx + rx * scale, cy + rz * scale, Math.max(2.5, b.r * scale), 0, PI * 2);\n                    ctx.stroke();\n                });\n\n                // Vektor pravca do Vukove piste\n                const dx = ARK2_AIRPORT.x - origin.x;\n                const dz = ARK2_AIRPORT.z - origin.z;\n                const rx = dx * cosH - dz * sinH;\n                const rz = dx * sinH + dz * cosH;\n                const len = Math.hypot(rx, rz) || 1;\n                const k = Math.min(54, len * scale) / len;\n\n                ctx.strokeStyle = '#4ade80';\n                ctx.fillStyle = '#4ade80';\n                ctx.lineWidth = 2;\n                ctx.beginPath();\n                ctx.moveTo(cx, cy);\n                ctx.lineTo(cx + rx * k, cy + rz * k);\n                ctx.stroke();\n                ctx.beginPath();\n                ctx.arc(cx + rx * k, cy + rz * k, 4.2, 0, PI * 2);\n                ctx.fill();\n\n                $('radar-readout').innerHTML = `NORVEŠKI AERODROM: <b style=\"color:#4ade80\">${Math.round(Math.hypot(dx, dz))}m<\/b>`;\n            } else {\n                // Kamp (narandžasto) i Avion (belo)\n                plotBlip(CAMP_POS.x, CAMP_POS.z, '#fb923c', 4.5);\n                plotBlip(PLANE_START_POS.x, PLANE_START_POS.z, '#f8fafc', 4.2);\n\n                let nearestPartDist = Infinity;\n                interactables.forEach((it) => {\n                    if (it.collected) return;\n                    if (it.type === 'part') {\n                        const d = Math.hypot(it.pos.x - origin.x, it.pos.z - origin.z);\n                        if (d < nearestPartDist) nearestPartDist = d;\n                        plotBlip(it.pos.x, it.pos.z, it.kind === 'fuel' ? '#f87171' : '#38bdf8', 4.0);\n                    } else if (activeChar === 'petar') {\n                        if (it.type === 'wood') plotBlip(it.pos.x, it.pos.z, '#fde047', 2.6);\n                        if (it.type === 'batt') plotBlip(it.pos.x, it.pos.z, '#4ade80', 2.6);\n                    }\n                });\n\n                if (nearestPartDist < Infinity) {\n                    $('radar-readout').innerHTML = `NAJBLIŽI DEO: <b style=\"color:#fde047\">${Math.round(nearestPartDist)}m<\/b>`;\n                } else {\n                    $('radar-readout').innerHTML = `<b style=\"color:#4ade80\">SVI DELOVI SKUPLJENI!<\/b>`;\n                }\n            }\n\n            ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\n            ctx.beginPath();\n            ctx.arc(cx, cy, 3.2, 0, PI * 2);\n            ctx.fill();\n        }\n\n        function triggerEndGame(victory, message) {\n            missionState=STATE_COMPLETE;\n            if(victory){try{localStorage.removeItem(ELITE_SAVE_KEY);}catch(_){}}\n            gameOver = true;\n            try {\n                if (document.pointerLockElement) document.exitPointerLock();\n            } catch (e) {}\n\n            $('end-overlay').style.display = 'flex';\n            const titleEl = $('end-title');\n            titleEl.textContent = victory ? 'MISIJA USPEŠNA: SLETELI STE KOD VUKA!' : 'ARKTIČKA OLUJA VAS JE SAVLADALA';\n            titleEl.style.color = victory ? '#4ade80' : '#ef4444';\n            $('end-desc').textContent = message;\n\n            const fmt = (s) => `${Math.floor(s / 60)} min ${Math.round(s % 60)} s`;\n            $('end-stats').innerHTML = `\n                <strong>IZVEŠTAJ MISIJE (OSTRVO T-76.4 &rarr; VUKOVA BAZA):<\/strong><br>\n                • Vreme preživljavanja i popravke: <strong>${fmt(survivalTimeSec)}<\/strong> | Zdravlje Petra: <strong>${Math.round(petarHealth)}%<\/strong><br>\n                • Ugrađeni delovi aviona: <strong>${installedCount}/5<\/strong> | Preostala baterija lampe: <strong>${Math.round(battery)}%<\/strong><br>\n                • Trajanje leta kroz oluju: <strong>${fmt(flightTimeSec)}<\/strong> | Integritet trupa aviona: <strong>${Math.max(0, Math.round(planeHull))}%<\/strong> | Sudari: <strong>${collisionHits}<\/strong>\n            `;\n            if (engineGain) engineGain.gain.value = 0;\n        }\n\n        // ============================================================================\n        // 6. FIZIKA PREŽIVLJAVANJA (FAZA 1) I FIZIKA LETA AVIONOM (FAZA 2)\n        // ============================================================================\n        function arkStormTick(dt){\n            stormTimer-=dt;\n            if(!cacheFound&&Math.hypot(playerPos.x-CACHE_X,playerPos.z-CACHE_Z)<24){cacheFound=true;eliteToast('NAPUŠTENI KAMP U BLIZINI');setSubtitle('Napušten kamp prethodne ekspedicije! Proveri sanduk i okolna drva.');}\n            if(stormPhase==='idle'){\n                if(stormTimer<=0){stormPhase='warn';stormTimer=8;eliteToast('VETAR JAČA — OLUJA STIŽE');setSubtitle('Vetar jača i vidljivost pada! Vrati se do vatre i sačekaj da oluja prođe.');}\n            }else if(stormPhase==='warn'){\n                stormLevel=Math.min(.35,stormLevel+dt*.05);\n                if(stormTimer<=0){stormPhase='storm';stormTimer=45;eliteToast('SNEŽNA OLUJA');}\n            }else if(stormPhase==='storm'){\n                stormLevel=Math.min(1,stormLevel+dt*.12);\n                if(stormTimer<=0){stormPhase='fade';stormTimer=10;}\n            }else{\n                stormLevel=Math.max(0,stormLevel-dt*.1);\n                if(stormTimer<=0||stormLevel<=0){stormLevel=0;stormPhase='idle';stormTimer=150+Math.random()*90;eliteToast('OLUJA JE PROŠLA');}\n            }\n            if(scene&&scene.fog)scene.fog.density=stormBaseFog+stormLevel*.011;\n            if(windGainNode)windGainNode.gain.value=.13+stormLevel*.22;\n        }\n\n        function updateSurvivalPhase(dt, elapsed) {\n            const prof = CHAR_PROFILES[activeChar];\n            survivalTimeSec += dt;\n\n            // Velocity lives in WORLD space; exponential acceleration is stable at 30, 60 or 144 FPS.\n            const dz = Number(moveF) - Number(moveB);\n            const dx = Number(moveR) - Number(moveL);\n            const isMovingInput = (dx !== 0 || dz !== 0);\n            const len = Math.hypot(dx, dz) || 1;\n            const carryPenalty = (carriedParts.length > 0 && activeChar !== 'andrej') ? .80 : 1.0;\n            const forward = V3(0, 0, -1).applyAxisAngle(UP_AXIS, yaw);\n            const right = V3(1, 0, 0).applyAxisAngle(UP_AXIS, yaw);\n            const intended = V3(0, 0, 0)\n                .addScaledVector(forward, dz / len)\n                .addScaledVector(right, dx / len);\n            if(arkStamina<5)arkExhausted=true;\n            if(arkStamina>24)arkExhausted=false;\n            arkActuallyRunning = isRunning && isMovingInput && !arkExhausted;\n            const baseSpeed = (prof.speed / 10) * (arkActuallyRunning ? 1.37 : 1.0) * carryPenalty;\n            const snowResistance = Math.max(.73, 1.0 - Math.max(0,getTerrainHeight(playerPos.x,playerPos.z))*0.018);\n            const targetV = intended.multiplyScalar(baseSpeed * snowResistance * (arkPickup ? .06 : 1));\n            const movingRate = isMovingInput ? (arkActuallyRunning ? 4.6 : 7.0) : 9.2;\n            velocity.x = arkApproach(velocity.x, targetV.x, movingRate, dt);\n            velocity.z = arkApproach(velocity.z, targetV.z, movingRate, dt);\n            arkMotionStamina(dt, isMovingInput, arkActuallyRunning);\n            const moveVec = V3(velocity.x * dt,0,velocity.z * dt);\n            const hitTree=(x,z)=>treeColliders.some(c=>Math.hypot(x-c.x,z-c.z)<c.r+0.16);\n            // Sweep one axis at a time so sliding next to a tree does not freeze the character.\n            const nx=THREE.MathUtils.clamp(playerPos.x+moveVec.x,-78,78);\n            if(!hitTree(nx,playerPos.z)) playerPos.x=nx;else velocity.x=0;\n            const nz=THREE.MathUtils.clamp(playerPos.z+moveVec.z,-78,78);\n            if(!hitTree(playerPos.x,nz)) playerPos.z=nz;else velocity.z=0;\n\n            const terrainY = getTerrainHeight(playerPos.x, playerPos.z);\n            playerPos.y += (terrainY - playerPos.y) * Math.min(1, dt * 14);\n            CHAR_PROFILES[activeChar].pos.set(playerPos.x, playerPos.y + 1.8, playerPos.z);\n\n            const moveSpeed = Math.hypot(velocity.x, velocity.z);\n            // Footstep cadence and sounds are distance based, not FPS based. See arkMotionFrame.\n\n            // Ažuriraj 3D model aktivnog lika (prirodno okretanje u smeru kretanja, glava prati pogled kamere)\n            const activeModel = companions[activeChar];\n            if (activeModel) {\n                activeModel.visible = thirdPersonMode;\n                activeModel.position.copy(playerPos);\n                if (isMovingInput && moveVec.lengthSq() > 0.00001) {\n                    const desiredFacing = Math.atan2(moveVec.x, moveVec.z);\n                    let diff = desiredFacing - activeCharFacing;\n                    while (diff > PI) diff -= PI * 2;\n                    while (diff < -PI) diff += PI * 2;\n                    activeCharFacing += diff * Math.min(1, dt * 11.5);\n                } else {\n                    const camLookYaw = yaw + PI;\n                    let diff = camLookYaw - activeCharFacing;\n                    while (diff > PI) diff -= PI * 2;\n                    while (diff < -PI) diff += PI * 2;\n                    if (Math.abs(diff) > 0.85) {\n                        activeCharFacing += diff * Math.min(1, dt * 4.5);\n                    }\n                }\n                activeModel.rotation.y = activeCharFacing;\n\n                // Okretanje glave aktivnog lika u pravcu kamere\n                let headDiff = (yaw + PI) - activeCharFacing;\n                while (headDiff > PI) headDiff -= PI * 2;\n                while (headDiff < -PI) headDiff += PI * 2;\n                activeModel.userData.lookYaw = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(headDiff * 0.55, -0.65, 0.65);\n                activeModel.userData.lookPitch = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(-pitch * 0.45, -0.35, 0.35);\n\n                charAnimBlend.interact = Math.max(0, charAnimBlend.interact - dt);\n                const animState = charAnimBlend.interact > 0 ? 'interact' : (moveSpeed > 0.75 ? (arkActuallyRunning ? 'run' : 'walk') : (playerWarmth < 42 ? 'cold' : 'idle'));\n                animateCharacter(activeModel, elapsed, animState);\n            }\n\n            // KAMERA TREĆEG LICA SA KOLIZIJOM I GLATKIM PRAĆENJEM (ili 1. lice na [V])\n            if (thirdPersonMode) {\n                const dynTargetDist = targetCamDist + (arkActuallyRunning && moveSpeed > 1.2 ? 0.55 : 0) - (focusedItem ? 0.45 : 0);\n                camDist += (dynTargetDist - camDist) * Math.min(1, dt * 7.5);\n\n                const clampedPitch = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(pitch, -0.75, 0.82);\n                const shoulderRight = right.clone().multiplyScalar(0.38);\n                const pivot = V3(playerPos.x, playerPos.y + 1.56, playerPos.z).add(shoulderRight);\n\n                const camBackDir = V3(\n                    Math.sin(yaw) * Math.cos(clampedPitch),\n                    -Math.sin(clampedPitch) + 0.22,\n                    Math.cos(yaw) * Math.cos(clampedPitch)\n                ).normalize();\n\n                // Provera kolizije kamere sa terenom, drvećem i trupom aviona\n                let safeDist = camDist;\n                for (let step = 0.25; step <= 1.0; step += 0.15) {\n                    const testD = camDist * step;\n                    const tx = pivot.x + camBackDir.x * testD;\n                    const ty = pivot.y + camBackDir.y * testD;\n                    const tz = pivot.z + camBackDir.z * testD;\n                    const gH = getTerrainHeight(tx, tz) + 0.48;\n                    if (ty < gH) {\n                        safeDist = Math.max(1.35, testD * 0.88);\n                        break;\n                    }\n                    const hitObstacle = treeColliders.some((c) => Math.hypot(tx - c.x, tz - c.z) < (c.r + 0.35));\n                    if (hitObstacle) {\n                        safeDist = Math.max(1.35, testD * 0.85);\n                        break;\n                    }\n                }\n\n                const desiredCamPos = pivot.clone().addScaledVector(camBackDir, safeDist);\n                const groundUnderCam = getTerrainHeight(desiredCamPos.x, desiredCamPos.z) + 0.52;\n                if (desiredCamPos.y < groundUnderCam) desiredCamPos.y = groundUnderCam;\n\n                smoothCamPos.lerp(desiredCamPos, Math.min(1, dt * 13.5));\n                const lookAhead = pivot.clone().addScaledVector(forward, 6.5).add(V3(0, Math.sin(clampedPitch) * 3.8, 0));\n                smoothLookAt.lerp(lookAhead, Math.min(1, dt * 16.0));\n\n                camera.position.copy(smoothCamPos);\n                camera.lookAt(smoothLookAt);\n            } else {\n                const headBob = moveSpeed > 0.6 ? Math.sin(elapsed * (arkActuallyRunning ? 14 : 9)) * 0.06 : 0;\n                camera.position.set(playerPos.x, playerPos.y + 1.78 + headBob, playerPos.z);\n                camera.rotation.set(pitch, yaw, 0);\n            }\n\n            // Potrošnja baterije i toplote (merena od pozicije aktivnog lika playerPos)\n            if (flashlightOn && battery > 0) {\n                battery = Math.max(0, battery - dt * 0.9 * prof.battDrain);\n                if (battery <= 0) {\n                    flashlightOn = false;\n                    setSubtitle(\"Baterija lampe se ispraznila! Potraži zelenu litijumsku bateriju ili se vrati do vatre.\");\n                }\n            }\n\n            arkStormTick(dt);\n            fireWarmth = Math.max(0, fireWarmth - dt * 0.68 * (1 + stormLevel * 0.9));\n            const distToFire = Math.hypot(playerPos.x - CAMP_POS.x, playerPos.z - CAMP_POS.z);\n            if (distToFire < 8.8 && fireWarmth > 10) {\n                playerWarmth = Math.min(100, playerWarmth + dt * 10.0);\n            } else {\n                playerWarmth = Math.max(0, playerWarmth - dt * (1.55 / prof.coldResist) * (isRunning ? 0.85 : 1.12) * (1 + stormLevel * 0.55));\n            }\n\n            petarHealth = fireWarmth < 20\n                ? Math.max(0, petarHealth - dt * 2.1)\n                : Math.min(100, petarHealth + dt * 0.65);\n\n            if (playerWarmth <= 0) {\n                triggerEndGame(false, `${prof.name} je podlegao hipotermiji u mećavi daleko od logorske vatre.`);\n                return;\n            }\n            if (petarHealth <= 0) {\n                triggerEndGame(false, \"Vatra u kampu se ugasila i Petar nije izdržao teške promrzline.\");\n                return;\n            }\n\n            // Detekcija najbližeg objekta za interakciju [E] u odnosu na aktivnog lika\n            focusedItem = null;\n            let bestDist = Infinity;\n            for (const it of interactables) {\n                if (it.collected || it._arkPicking || it.type === 'comp_' + activeChar) continue;\n                const d = Math.hypot(playerPos.x - it.pos.x, playerPos.z - it.pos.z);\n                if (d < it.radius && d < bestDist) {\n                    bestDist = d;\n                    focusedItem = it;\n                }\n            }\n\n            const promptEl = $('interact-prompt');\n            promptEl.style.display = focusedItem ? 'block' : 'none';\n            if (focusedItem) {\n                promptEl.textContent = focusedItem.label();\n            }\n            $('reticle').classList.toggle('active', !!focusedItem);\n\n            if (engineGain) {\n                engineGain.gain.value = planeReady ? 0.03 : 0.0;\n            }\n\n            arkMotionFrame(dt,elapsed,moveSpeed,isMovingInput);\n            updateSurvivalHUD();\n            drawRadar();\n        }\n\n        function damageAircraft(amount, msg) {\n            planeHull = Math.max(0, planeHull - amount);\n            hitCooldown = 1.3;\n            collisionHits++;\n            playSfx('crash');\n            setSubtitle(msg || `UDAR U LEDENI BREG! Integritet trupa aviona: ${Math.round(planeHull)}%`);\n        }\n\n        function updateFlightPhase(dt,elapsed){ark2FlightUpdate(dt,elapsed);}\n\n        \n\n        // ============================================================================\n        // 7. GLAVNA ANIMACIONA PETLJA\n        // ============================================================================\n        function animate() {\n            if (window.arkStop3D) return;\n            requestAnimationFrame(animate);\n\n            const now = performance.now();\n            const dt = Math.min((now - prevTime) / 1000, 0.1);\n            const elapsed = now * 0.001;\n            prevTime = now;\n            if(elitePaused){eliteFrame(0,now);return;}\n\n            if (missionState === STATE_LOADING) updateLoading(now);\n            if (gameStarted && !gameOver && !elitePaused) {\n                switch (missionState) {\n                    case STATE_INTRO_CUTSCENE: updateIntroCutscene(dt,elapsed); break;\n                    case STATE_SURVIVAL: updateSurvivalPhase(dt,elapsed); break;\n                    case STATE_TAKEOFF_CUTSCENE: updateTakeoffCutscene(dt,elapsed); break;\n                    case STATE_FLIGHT: updateFlightPhase(dt,elapsed); break;\n                    case STATE_PHASE3_READY: ark2ReadyRoam(dt,elapsed); break;\n                    case STATE_OUTRO_CUTSCENE: updateOutroCutscene(dt,elapsed); break;\n                }\n            }\n\n            const fireNorm = fireWarmth / 100;\n            const camLookDir = V3(0, 0, -1).applyQuaternion(camera.quaternion);\n            sharedUniforms.time.value = elapsed;\n            sharedUniforms.fireIntensity.value = fireNorm;\n\n            if (missionState === STATE_SURVIVAL) {\n                const lampOrigin = thirdPersonMode\n                    ? V3(playerPos.x, playerPos.y + 1.38, playerPos.z).addScaledVector(camLookDir, 0.35)\n                    : camera.position.clone();\n                flashlight.position.copy(lampOrigin);\n                flashlight.target.position.copy(lampOrigin).addScaledVector(camLookDir, 22);\n                const effPower = flashlightOn ? Math.max(0.15, battery / 100) : 0;\n                flashlight.intensity = 5.4 * effPower;\n                sharedUniforms.flashlightPower.value = effPower;\n                sharedUniforms.lightPos.value.copy(lampOrigin);\n                sharedUniforms.lightDir.value.copy(camLookDir);\n            }\n\n            campfireLight.intensity = (2.8 + Math.sin(elapsed * 15) * 0.45) * fireNorm;\n            planeStrobeLight.intensity = gamePhase === 'survival' ? (Math.sin(elapsed * 6) > 0.3 ? 2.8 : 0.1) : 0;\n\n            fireFlames.forEach((f, idx) => {\n                f.scale.set(\n                    Math.max(0.1, fireNorm),\n                    Math.max(0.1, fireNorm * (0.85 + Math.sin(elapsed * 12 + idx * 2) * 0.25) * 1.4),\n                    Math.max(0.1, fireNorm)\n                );\n            });\n\n            partBeacons.forEach((orb, idx) => {\n                orb.position.y = 0.92 + Math.sin(elapsed * 3.5 + idx) * 0.15;\n            });\n\n            if (propellerGroup && propellerGroup.visible && (missionState === STATE_SURVIVAL || (missionState === STATE_FLIGHT && !ark2.landed))) {\n                const rpm = gamePhase === 'flight' ? (32 + throttle * 32) : (planeReady ? 9 : 1.5);\n                propellerGroup.rotation.z -= dt * rpm;\n            }\n\n            for(const [key,model] of Object.entries(companions)){\n                if(key!==activeChar && model.visible && missionState===STATE_SURVIVAL){\n                    // Saborci u kampu okreću glavu ka aktivnom igraču kada priđe blizu\n                    const dx = playerPos.x - model.position.x;\n                    const dz = playerPos.z - model.position.z;\n                    if (Math.hypot(dx, dz) < 9.5) {\n                        const angToPlayer = Math.atan2(dx, dz) - model.rotation.y;\n                        model.userData.lookYaw = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(Math.atan2(Math.sin(angToPlayer), Math.cos(angToPlayer)) * 0.5, -0.55, 0.55);\n                    } else {\n                        model.userData.lookYaw = 0;\n                    }\n                    animateCharacter(model,elapsed,key==='petar'?'cold':'idle');\n                }\n            }\n            for(const pilot of planePilots)animateCharacter(pilot,elapsed,'pilot');\n            for(const passenger of planePassengers)animateCharacter(passenger,elapsed,'sit');\n            if(vukCharacter)animateCharacter(vukCharacter,elapsed,'wave');\n            if(planeWarningBulb)planeWarningBulb.visible=(missionState===STATE_INTRO_CUTSCENE && cinematicTime>=14 && cinematicTime<22);\n            if (vukWavingArm) {\n                vukWavingArm.rotation.z = -0.5 + Math.sin(elapsed * 5.5) * 0.45;\n            }\n            if (towerBeaconGroup) {\n                towerBeaconGroup.rotation.y += dt * 2.4;\n            }\n\n            // Animacija 8.000 čestica snega\n            const snowArr = snowParticles.geometry.attributes.position.array;\n            const cx = camera.position.x, cy = camera.position.y, cz = camera.position.z;\n            const minY = gamePhase === 'survival' ? 0 : cy - 18;\n            const maxY = gamePhase === 'survival' ? 26 : cy + 22;\n\n            for (let i = 0, v = 0; i < eliteActiveSnow * 3; i += 3, v++) {\n                const sv = snowVelocities[v];\n                snowArr[i]     += (sv[0] * (0.65 + arkWindGust * 1.15 + stormLevel * 2.2) + Math.sin(elapsed + v * 0.31) * 0.012) * dt * 60;\n                snowArr[i + 1] += sv[1] * dt * 60;\n                snowArr[i + 2] += (sv[2]+Math.sin(elapsed*.4+v)*.006) * dt * 60;\n\n                if (snowArr[i + 1] < minY || snowArr[i + 1] > maxY + 8 || Math.abs(snowArr[i] - cx) > 55 || Math.abs(snowArr[i + 2] - cz) > 55) {\n                    snowArr[i]     = cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 100;\n                    snowArr[i + 1] = maxY - Math.random() * 4;\n                    snowArr[i + 2] = cz + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 100;\n                }\n            }\n            snowParticles.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;\n\n            // Misteriozne siluete u magli tokom Faze 1\n            silhouettes.forEach((s, i) => {\n                if (missionState !== STATE_SURVIVAL) {\n                    s.group.visible = false;\n                    return;\n                }\n                s.angle += dt * 0.08 * (i % 2 ? -1 : 1);\n                const sx = cx + Math.cos(s.angle) * s.dist;\n                const sz = cz + Math.sin(s.angle) * s.dist;\n                s.group.position.set(sx, getTerrainHeight(sx, sz), sz);\n                s.group.lookAt(cx, cy, cz);\n\n                const toSil = V3(sx - cx, 0, sz - cz).normalize();\n                if (flashlightOn && camLookDir.dot(toSil) > 0.92) {\n                    s.dist = Math.min(54, s.dist + dt * 18);\n                } else {\n                    s.dist = Math.max(26, s.dist - dt * 1.5);\n                }\n            });\n\n            if (windFilter) {\n                windFilter.frequency.value = 280 + Math.sin(elapsed * 0.7) * 110 + stormLevel * 260;\n            }\n\n            eliteFrame(dt, now);\n            try { renderer.render(scene, camera); }\n            catch (err) { window.arkStop3D = true; if (window.arkShowError) window.arkShowError(err, 'GREŠKA 3D PRIKAZA'); else console.error(err); }\n        }\n\n        // ============================================================================\n        // ARKTIČKI RUŠET // ELITE EDITION — non-destructive gameplay enhancements.\n        // No external resources, no new Three.js version, and no save migration.\n        // ============================================================================\n        const ELITE_SAVE_KEY='arkticki_ruset_elite_save_v2';\n        const ELITE_SETTINGS_KEY='arkticki_ruset_elite_settings_v1';\n        const ELITE_QUALITIES={\n            low:{dpr:0.72,snow:1800,shadows:512},\n          pitch=eliteClamp(Number(saved.pitch)||0,-1.25,1.25);\n                thirdPersonMode=Boolean(saved.thirdPersonMode);\n                const eliteGetWaypoint(){\n            if(missionState===STATE_FLIGHT && !ark2.landed)return {name:'NORVEŠKI AERODROM',pos:ARK2_AIRPORT,color:'#4ade80'};\n            if(missionState!==STATE_SURVIVAL)return       if(carriedParts.length>0||planeReady)return {name:planeReady?'UKRCAJ TIM':'UGRADI DELOVE',pos:PLANE_START_POS,color:'#7dd3fc'};\n            const }\n            if(fireWarmth<40&&woodCount===0){\n                const wood=interactables.filter(it=>it.type==='wood'&&!it.collected)\n                    .sort((a,b)=>a.pos.distanceToSquared(playerPos)-b.pos.distanceToSquared(playerPos));\n                if(wood.length)return{name:'PRONAĐI DRVA',pos:wood[0].pos,color:'#fb923c'};\n            }\n            return {name:'VRATI SE DO AVIONA',pos:PLANE_START_POS,color:'#7dd3fc'};\n        }\n        function eliteFrame(dt,now){\n            if(!renderer)return;\n                if(eliteFps<27)eliteQualityScale=Math.max(.68,eliteQualityScale-.09);\n                    else if(eliteFps>56)eliteQualityScale=Math.min(1,eliteQualityScale+.045);\n                    if(Math.abs(old-eliteQualityScale)>.01&&now-eliteLastResize>2500){eliteLastResize=now;eliteApplyQuality('auto');}\n                }\n            }\n       if(Math.abs(camera.fov-targetFov)>.03){camera.fov+=(targetFov-camera.fov)*Math.min(1,dt*2.1);camera.updateProjectionMatrix();}\n            }\n            if(now-eliteLastHud>145){\n g.translate(64,64);\n                // boot tread composed of a soft compressed snow bed and dark sole marks\n                g.fillStyle='#b9d7ec';\n                g.shadowBlur=11;g.shadowColor='rgba(45,80,120,.52)';\n                g.beginPath();g.ellipse(0,4,29,47,0,0,Math.PI*2);g.fill();\n                g.shadowBlur=0;g.fillStyle='rgba(23,58,91,.38)';\n                for(let y=-27;y<34;y+=10)g.fillRect(-21,y,42,4);\n                g.fillStyle='rgba(231,242,255,.28)';\n                g.beginPath();g.ellipse(-4,-30,15,8,0,0,Math.PI*2);g.fill();\n        grad.addColorStop(1,'rgba(221,244,255,0)');g.fillStyle=grad;g.fillRect(0,0,128,128);\n            }\n            const tex=new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);\n        }\n        function arkFootprintAt(pos,dir){\n            if(!arkFootprints || missionState!==STATE_SURVIVAL)return;\n            const angle=Math.atan2(dir.x,dir.z);\n            const       }\n        function arkMotionFrame(dt,elapsed,speed,isMoving){\n            arkWindGust=arkApproach(arkWindGust,.5+.5*Math.sin(elapsed*.46+Math.sin(elapsed*.17)*2),.65,dt);\n function utf8(s){s=unescape(encodeURIComponent(s));var b=new Uint8Array(s.length);for(var i=0;i<s.length;i++)b[i]=s.charCodeAt(i);return b;}
function sha256(msg){var b=utf8(msg),l=b.length,n=((l+9+63)>>6)<<6,w=new Uint8Array(n),H=H0.slice(),W=new Array(64),i,o;w.set(b);w[l]=0x80;var bits=l*8;w[n-4]=(bits>>>24)&255;w[n-3]=(bits>>>16)&255;w[n-2]=(bits>>>8)&255;w[n-1]=bits&255;
 for(o=0;o<n;o+=64){for(i=0;i<16;i++)W[i]=(w[o+4*i]<<24)|(w[o+4*i+1]<<16)|(w[o+4*i+2]<<8)|w[o+4*i+3];
  for(i=16;i<64;i++){var x=W[i-15],y=W[i-2],s0=((x>>>7)|(x<<25))^((x>>>18)|(x<<14))^(x>>>3),s1=((y>>>17)|(y<<15))^((y>>>19)|(y<<13))^(y>>>10);W[i]=(W[i-16]+s0+W[i-7]+s1)|0;}
  var a=H[0],bb=H[1],c=H[2],d=H[3],e=H[4],f=H[5],g=H[6],h=H[7];
  for(i=0;i<64;i++){var S1=((e>>>6)|(e<<26))^((e>>>11)|(e<<21))^((e>>>25)|(e<<7)),ch=(e&f)^(~e&g),t1=(h+S1+ch+K[i]+W[i])|0,S0=((a>>>2)|(a<<30))^((a>>>13)|(a<<19))^((a>>>22)|(a<<10)),mj=(a&bb)^(a&c)^(bb&c),t2=(S0+mj)|0;h=g;g=f;f=e;e=(d+t1)|0;d=c;c=bb;bb=a;a=(t1+t2)|0;}
  H[0]=(H[0]+a)|0;H[1]=(H[1]+bb)|0;H[2]=(H[2]+c)|0;H[3]=(H[3]+d)|0;H[4]=(H[4]+e)|0;H[5]=(H[5]+f)|0;H[6]=(H[6]+g)|0;H[7]=(H[7]+h)|0;}
 return H.map(function(v){return ('00000000'+(v>>>0).toString(16)).slice(-8);}).join('');}

function toast(t){var r=rootEl();if(!r)return;var old=r.querySelector('.kb-dlc-toast');if(old)old.remove();var d=document.createElement('div');d.className='kb-dlc-toast';d.setAttribute('role','status');d.textContent=t;r.appendChild(d);setTimeout(function(){if(d.parentNode)d.remove();},5200);}
function currentMain(){var r=rootEl();return r&&(r.querySelector('main')||r);}
function decorate(main,name){try{css();if(!main)return;
 Array.prototype.forEach.call(main.querySelectorAll('.kb-dlc-tag,.kb-dlc-row'),function(n){n.remove();});
 var any=false,locked=false;
 Array.prototype.forEach.call(main.querySelectorAll('.kb-mode-card[data-action^="launch-"]'),function(c){var m=c.getAttribute('data-action').slice(7),id=prodFor(m);if(!id)return;any=true;var p=CFG.products[id],own_=has(id);if(!own_)locked=true;c.classList.toggle('kb-dlc-locked',!own_);
  var box=c.querySelector('.kb-mode-content');if(!box)return;var t=document.createElement('span');t.className='kb-dlc-tag'+(own_?' kb-dlc-own':'');t.textContent=own_?'✓ DLC OTKLJUČAN':'🔒 DLC · '+p.price;box.insertBefore(t,box.firstChild);});
 if(any&&name==='solo'){var g=main.querySelector('.kb-mode-grid');if(g){var row=document.createElement('div');row.className='kb-dlc-row';row.innerHTML=locked?'<button type="button" class="kb-btn" data-action="dlc-open">DLC · kupi ili unesi kod</button><span class="kb-muted kb-small">2D igra je besplatna. 3D svet, Noćni lov i Arktički rušet su DLC.</span>':'<span class="kb-muted kb-small">✓ Sav DLC je otključan.</span>';g.parentNode.insertBefore(row,g.nextSibling);}}
 if(welcome){var w=welcome;welcome=null;setTimeout(function(){toast('Hvala na kupovini! Otključano: '+w+'.');},300);}
}catch(e){try{S.errors&&S.errors.push('dlc:'+e.message);}catch(_){}}}

var pw=null;
function closePaywall(){if(pw){try{pw.d.close();}catch(_){}try{pw.d.remove();}catch(_){}pw=null;}}
function firstLocked(){var r=null;Object.keys(CFG.products).forEach(function(id){if(!r&&!has(id))r=id;});return r;}
function licenseOn(){var L=CFG.license;if(!L||!L.enabled)return false;return Object.keys(L.productIds).some(function(k){return L.productIds[k];});}
function verifyLicense(key,cb){var ctrl=window.AbortController?new AbortController():null,t=setTimeout(function(){if(ctrl)ctrl.abort();},10000),body=new URLSearchParams();body.set('license_key',key);
 fetch('https://api.lemonsqueezy.com/v1/licenses/validate',{method:'POST',headers:{'Accept':'application/json','Content-Type':'application/x-www-form-urlencoded'},body:body.toString(),signal:ctrl?ctrl.signal:undefined}).then(function(r){return r.json();}).then(function(j){clearTimeout(t);if(j&&j.valid&&j.meta){var pid=String(j.meta.product_id),hit=null;Object.keys(CFG.license.productIds).forEach(function(id){if(CFG.license.productIds[id]&&String(CFG.license.productIds[id])===pid)hit=id;});if(hit){grant(hit,'license');return cb(null,hit);}}cb('bad');}).catch(function(){clearTimeout(t);cb('net');});}
function normalize(s){s=String(s||'').trim().toLowerCase();try{s=s.normalize('NFD').replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g,'');}catch(_){}return s.replace(/\s+/g,' ');}
function afterUnlock(msgEl,pending){if(msgEl){msgEl.style.color='#9fe3b5';msgEl.textContent='Otključano. Hvala!';}decorate(currentMain(),(rootEl()||{}).dataset&&rootEl().dataset.view);setTimeout(function(){closePaywall();if(pending){var b=document.querySelector('#kb-shell [data-action="launch-'+pending+'"]');if(b)b.click();}},700);}
function openPaywall(id,pending){var r=rootEl();if(!r)return;css();closePaywall();var p=CFG.products[id];if(!p)return;
 var d=document.createElement('dialog');d.className='kb-dialog kb-dlc';d.setAttribute('aria-label',p.name);
 d.innerHTML='<span class="kb-dlc-kicker">DLC</span><h2>'+esc(p.name)+'</h2><p>'+esc(p.desc)+'</p><ul class="kb-dlc-list">'+p.includes.map(function(x){return '<li>'+esc(x)+'</li>';}).join('')+'</ul><div class="kb-dlc-price">'+esc(p.price)+'<small> jednokratno</small></div><div class="kb-row"><button type="button" class="kb-btn kb-primary" data-dlc="buy">Kupi karticom</button><button type="button" class="kb-btn" data-dlc="close">Zatvori</button></div><form class="kb-dlc-form" autocomplete="off"><label for="kb-dlc-code">Imaš kod ili ključ?</label><div class="kb-dlc-inputrow"><input id="kb-dlc-code" name="kbcode" type="text" autocapitalize="off" autocomplete="off" autocorrect="off" spellcheck="false" placeholder="Unesi ovde"><button type="submit" class="kb-btn">Otključaj</button></div><p class="kb-dlc-msg" role="status" aria-live="polite"></p></form>';
 r.appendChild(d);var msg=d.querySelector('.kb-dlc-msg'),input=d.querySelector('#kb-dlc-code'),fails=0,blockedUntil=0;pw={d:d,id:id};
 function say(t,bad){msg.style.color=bad?'#f0a79a':'';msg.textContent=t;}
 d.addEventListener('click',function(e){var b=e.target.closest('[data-dlc]');if(!b)return;e.stopPropagation();var k=b.getAttribute('data-dlc');if(k==='close'){closePaywall();return;}
  if(k==='buy'){if(!p.buyUrl){say('Plaćanje karticom još nije podešeno u ovoj verziji igre.',true);return;}var w=null;try{w=window.open(p.buyUrl,'_blank','noopener');}catch(_){}if(!w)location.href=p.buyUrl;else say('Plati u novom prozoru. Po povratku se igra otključava sama.');}});
 d.addEventListener('close',function(){if(pw&&pw.d===d)pw=null;try{d.remove();}catch(_){}});
 d.querySelector('form').addEventListener('submit',function(e){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();var raw=input.value;if(!normalize(raw)){say('Upiši kod.',true);return;}if(Date.now()<blockedUntil){say('Previše pokušaja. Sačekaj malo.',true);return;}
  var h=sha256(normalize(raw));if(CFG.promoHashes.indexOf(h)>=0){grantAll('promo');afterUnlock(msg,pending);return;}
  var bad=function(){fails++;if(fails>=5){blockedUntil=Date.now()+15000;fails=0;say('Previše pokušaja. Sačekaj 15 s.',true);}else say('Kod nije ispravan.',true);};
  if(licenseOn()){say('Proveravam ključ…');verifyLicense(raw.trim(),function(err){if(!err)afterUnlock(msg,pending);else if(err==='net')say('Ne mogu da proverim ključ (nema veze). Probaj ponovo.',true);else bad();});return;}
  bad();});
 d.showModal();setTimeout(function(){try{input.blur();}catch(_){}},0);}
S.dlcGate=function(mode){var id=prodFor(mode);if(!id||has(id))return true;openPaywall(id,mode);return false;};
S.dlcAction=function(a){if(a==='dlc-open'){var id=firstLocked();if(id)openPaywall(id,null);else toast('Sav DLC je već otključan.');return true;}return false;};
function refresh(){own=readStore();decorate(currentMain(),(rootEl()||{}).dataset&&rootEl().dataset.view);if(pw&&has(pw.id)){var m=pw.d.querySelector('.kb-dlc-msg');afterUnlock(m,null);toast('DLC je otključan.');}}
window.addEventListener('storage',function(e){if(e.key===KEY)refresh();});
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',function(){if(!document.hidden)refresh();});
})(window.KBShell);
