function focusables(scope){return Array.from((scope||root).querySelectorAll('button,input,select,textarea,summary,a[href],canvas[tabindex]')).filter(function(e){return !e.disabled&&e.getClientRects().length&&!e.closest('[hidden]');});}
S.handleKey=function(e){if(!root)return;if(root.hidden){if(e.type==='keydown'&&e.key==='Escape'&&window.KBPhone3D&&window.KBPhone3D.isOpen()){e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();window.KBPhone3D.close();return;}if(e.type==='keydown'&&(e.key==='Escape'||e.key.toLowerCase()==='p')&&B.mode()&&!/INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT/.test(e.target.tagName)){e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();pause();}return;}e.stopImmediatePropagation();if(e.type!=='keydown')return;var d=root.querySelector('dialog[open]');if(d){if(e.key==='Escape'){e.preventDefault();d.close();}else if(e.key==='Tab'){var bs=focusables(d),i=bs.indexOf(document.activeElement);if(e.shiftKey&&i<=0){e.preventDefault();bs[bs.length-1].focus();}else if(!e.shiftKey&&i===bs.length-1){e.preventDefault();bs[0].focus();}}return;}if(view==='gate'&&!['Tab','Shift','Control','Alt','Meta'].includes(e.key)){e.preventDefault();enter();return;}if(e.key==='Escape'){e.preventDefault();back();return;}var f=focusables();if(e.key==='Tab'&&f.length){var i=f.indexOf(document.activeElement);if(!e.shiftKey&&i===f.length-1||e.shiftKey&&i<=0){e.preventDefault();f[e.shiftKey?f.length-1:0].focus();}}if(e.target.tagName==='CANVAS'&&['ArrowLeft','ArrowRight'].includes(e.key)){e.preventDefault();e.target.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('kb-turn',{detail:e.key==='ArrowLeft'?-.16:.16}));}};
function enter(){if(view!=='gate'&&view!=='intro')return;A.init(settings);if(view==='intro'||settings.motion||D.read('seen_intro',false)){render('home',true);return;}clean();view='intro';root.dataset.view='intro';main.innerHTML='<div class="kb-intro">'+heading('PALILULA / 23:47','„Brate, samo da pogledamo<br>šta je tamo.“','Andrej, Pera i Lazar. Poslednja svetla ostaju iza vas.')+button('Preskoči uvod '+icon('arrow'),'skip','kb-primary')+'</div>';main.querySelector('button').focus();later(function(){try{D.write('seen_intro',true);}catch(_){}render('home',true);},5000);}
function loader(){var labels=[['Podaci mape','Proveravamo mapu'],['Teksture','Portreti i pozadina'],['Zvuk','Čeka tvoj dodir'],['Svet','Proveravamo pokretanje']],done=0,failed=0;main.innerHTML='<div class="kb-loading"><span class="kb-eyebrow">POVRATAK U KRAJ</span><h1>KALAČ BRDO<span>.</span></h1><div class="kb-loading-portraits">'+portrait(0)+portrait(1)+portrait(2)+'</div><div class="kb-progress-head"><b id="kb-load-title">Pripremamo noć.</b><span id="kb-load-count">0 / 4</span></div><progress value="0" max="4" aria-label="Priprema igre"></progress><ol class="kb-load-stages">'+labels.map(function(s,i){return '<li id="kb-load-'+i+'"><b>'+s[0]+'</b><small>'+s[1]+'</small></li>';}).join('')+'</ol><p class="kb-tip">'+tips[0]+'</p></div>';
function finish(i,msg,ok){var li=main.querySelector('#kb-load-'+i);if(!li)return;li.className=ok?'done':'degraded';li.querySelector('small').textContent=msg;done++;if(!ok)failed++;main.querySelector('progress').value=done;main.querySelector('#kb-load-count').textContent=done+' / 4';if(done===4){S.readyInMs=Math.round(performance.now());root.dataset.readyMs=String(S.readyInMs);view='gate';root.dataset.view='gate';main.querySelector('#kb-load-title').textContent=failed?'Spremno uz rezervni prikaz.':'Brdo je spremno.';var g=document.createElement('div');g.className='kb-load-gate';g.innerHTML=button('Dodirni ili pritisni bilo šta '+icon('arrow'),'enter','kb-primary')+'<small>Ovim uključuješ zvuk. Uvod možeš da preskočiš.</small>';main.querySelector('.kb-loading').appendChild(g);g.querySelector('button').focus({preventScroll:true});}}
var ti=0;timers.push(setInterval(function(){var p=main.querySelector('.kb-tip');if(p)p.textContent=tips[++ti%tips.length];},4300));setTimeout(function(){try{var data=JSON.parse(document.getElementById('kb-real-map-data').textContent);finish(0,data.meta.counts.road_sections+' deonica ulica · podaci spremni',true);}catch(_){finish(0,'Mapa nije dostupna · 2D ostaje dostupan',false);}},0);
Promise.all(['heroes.jpg','menu-art.png'].map(function(n){return new Promise(function(resolve){var im=new Image(),ended=false;function end(ok){if(ended)return;ended=true;clearTimeout(t);resolve(ok);}var t=setTimeout(function(){end(false);},2200);im.onload=function(){end(true);};im.onerror=function(){end(false);};im.src=S.asset(n);});})).then(function(r){finish(1,r.every(Boolean)?'Portreti i pozadina spremni':'Neki resursi nisu dostupni · rezervni prikaz',r.every(Boolean));});setTimeout(function(){finish(2,'Spreman nakon tvog dodira',true);finish(3,window.KBBeta3D?'3D i 2D spremni za pokretanje':'Dostupan rezervni 2D režim',!!window.KBBeta3D);},0);}
function init(){root=document.createElement('section');root.id='kb-shell';root.setAttribute('aria-label','Kalač Brdo — glavni meni');root.dataset.view='loader';root.innerHTML='<div class="kb-art" aria-hidden="true"></div><div class="kb-art-shade" aria-hidden="true"></div><header class="kb-header"><button data-action="home" class="kb-brand" aria-label="Glavni meni"><span class="kb-monogram">K<span>B</span></span><span>KALAČ BRDO<small>NOĆNA TAJNA</small></span></button><nav aria-label="Glavna navigacija">'+button('Igraj','play','kb-nav')+button('Likovi','roster','kb-nav')+button('Misije','missions','kb-nav')+button('O igri','about','kb-nav')+'</nav><div class="kb-header-tools">'+button(icon('sound'),'sound','kb-icon','aria-label="Isključi zvuk menija"')+button(icon('settings'),'settings','kb-icon','aria-label="Podešavanja"')+'</div></header><main class="kb-main"></main><footer class="kb-footer"><span><i></i> TVOJ KRAJ. TVOJA PRIČA.</span><div>'+button('O igri','about','kb-text-btn')+button('Podrška','support','kb-text-btn')+'<small>MENI 2.1</small></div></footer><p class="kb-toast" role="status" aria-live="polite"></p>';document.body.appendChild(root);main=root.querySelector('main');toast=root.querySelector('.kb-toast');returnButton=document.createElement('button');returnButton.id='kb-shell-return';returnButton.hidden=true;returnButton.innerHTML=icon('back')+' Meni';returnButton.setAttribute('aria-label','Pauziraj i otvori meni');document.body.appendChild(returnButton);returnButton.onclick=pause;visible();
root.addEventListener('click',function(e){if(e.target.closest('dialog'))return;var b=e.target.closest('[data-action],[data-hero]');if(!b||b.disabled)return;if(['loader','gate','intro'].includes(view)&&b.closest('.kb-header,.kb-footer'))return;if(b.dataset.hero!==undefined){heroIndex=Number(b.dataset.hero);render('roster',true);return;}action(b.dataset.action);});root.addEventListener('submit',function(e){e.preventDefault();});
root.addEventListener('input',function(e){var el=e.target;if(el.name&&el.closest('#kb-character-form')){remember();draft[el.name]=el.type==='range'?Number(el.value):el.value;var out=main.querySelector('#kb-height');if(out)out.textContent=draft.height+' cm';main.querySelector('#kb-preview-name').textContent=draft.name;if(stage)stage.setCharacter(draft);var u=main.querySelector('[data-action="undo"]');if(u)u.disabled=false;}if(el.dataset.setting){var k=el.dataset.setting;settings[k]=el.type==='checkbox'?el.checked:(k==='volume'||k==='text')?Number(el.value):el.value;try{D.write('settings',settings);if(k==='quality')B.quality(settings);apply();var v=main.querySelector('#kb-volume');if(v)v.textContent=settings.volume+'%';}catch(err){notice(err.message);}}});
root.addEventListener('change',function(e){if(e.target.id!=='kb-save-file')return;var f=e.target.files[0];if(!f)return;if(f.size>2097152){notice('Datoteka je prevelika (najviše 2 MB).');return;}f.text().then(function(text){if(view!=='support')return;try{pendingImport=D.parseSaves(text);main.querySelector('#kb-import-preview').innerHTML='<p>Provereno '+Object.keys(pendingImport.values).length+' stavki. Potvrdi zamenu da nastaviš.</p>'+button('Pregledaj i potvrdi uvoz','confirm-import','kb-primary');}catch(err){pendingImport=null;notice(err.message);}}).catch(function(){notice('Datoteka nije mogla da se pročita.');});});
root.addEventListener('pointermove',function(e){if(settings.motion||e.pointerType==='touch'||view!=='home')return;root.style.setProperty('--kb-px',((e.clientX/innerWidth-.5)*9)+'px');root.style.setProperty('--kb-py',((e.clientY/innerHeight-.5)*6)+'px');});root.addEventListener('kb-stage-lost',function(){notice('3D pregled je prekinut. Otvori ponovo ovaj ekran za novi pregled.');});
S.startGame=function(o){launch(o&&o.mode||'free');};S.launchExtension=function(action){var snapshot=window.KBMissionDirector.instance.serialize();hide();try{if(B.mode()!=='beta')B.start({mode:'continue-free'},settings);window.KBMissionDirector.instance.restore(snapshot,true);window.KBStoryExtensions.launch(action);}catch(e){render('home',true);notice(e.message||'Pokušaj nije pokrenut.');}};
S.open=function(){if(B.mode())pause();else render('home');};S.leaveHorror=function(){returnButton.hidden=true;render('home',true);};S.getHeroes=function(){return D.clone(D.heroes);};S.getCharacter=function(){return chosen&&D.clone(chosen);};S.setStageAdapter=function(adapter){if(!adapter||typeof adapter.mount!=='function')throw Error('Stage adapter mora da ima mount funkciju.');S.modules.stage=adapter;};
padTimer=setInterval(function(){if(root.hidden||document.hidden||window.KBMissionBoardA2?.isOpen||window.KBStoryExtensions?.modal||!navigator.getGamepads)return;var g=Array.from(navigator.getGamepads()).find(Boolean);if(!g)return;var down=g.buttons.some(function(b,i){return [0,1,12,13,14,15].includes(i)&&b.pressed;})||Math.abs(g.axes[1]||0)>.65;if(!down){padHeld=false;return;}if(padHeld&&performance.now()-lastPad<240)return;padHeld=true;lastPad=performance.now();if(view==='gate'){enter();return;}var d=root.querySelector('dialog[open]');if(g.buttons[1]&&g.buttons[1].pressed){if(d)d.close();else back();return;}if(g.buttons[0]&&g.buttons[0].pressed){if(document.activeElement&&root.contains(document.activeElement)&&document.activeElement.click)document.activeElement.click();return;}var f=focusables(d||root);if(!f.length)return;var i=f.indexOf(document.activeElement),dir=(g.buttons[12]&&g.buttons[12].pressed)||(g.buttons[14]&&g.buttons[14].pressed)||(g.axes[1]||0)<-.65?-1:1;f[(i+dir+f.length)%f.length].focus();},100);
window.addEventListener('pagehide',function(){clearInterval(padTimer);clean();});var nativePause=document.getElementById('betaPause');if(nativePause)new MutationObserver(function(){if(nativePause.hidden)nativePass=false;else if(!nativePass&&root.hidden&&window.KBBeta3D&&KBBeta3D.live&&KBBeta3D.status.paused)pause();}).observe(nativePause,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['hidden','style','class']});var gameHost=document.getElementById('gm');if(gameHost)new MutationObserver(function(){if(root.hidden&&gameHost.hidden&&!B.mode()){nativePass=false;render('home',true);returnButton.hidden=true;}}).observe(gameHost,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['hidden']});document.documentElement.classList.remove('kb-shell-boot');loader();}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init,{once:true});else init();})(window.KBShell);
